## Security Review Findings

---

### Finding 1 — Weak/Placeholder API Key in `.env.mcp`

**File:** `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.env.mcp`, line 22

**Severity: High**

The file contains a hardcoded, placeholder API key:

```
REDACTED
```

The inline comment even acknowledges it ("change in production!"), meaning this value has been shipped as-is. If `.env.mcp` is ever committed or if any code uses this key in a deployed environment, it provides zero authentication — any caller who reads the comment could supply this key.

**Fix:** Generate a cryptographically random secret (e.g., `python3 -c "import secrets; print(secrets.token_hex(32))"`) and set it in the actual deployment environment, not in the file. Rotate to a real secret before any deployment. Confirm `.env.mcp` is in `.gitignore` (it is not currently listed there — see Finding 2).

---

### Finding 2 — `.env.mcp` Not Listed in `.gitignore`

**File:** `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.env.mcp` (root of repo)

**Severity: High**

The `.gitignore` has no entry covering `.env.mcp`. The `.gitignore` covers `data/`, `*.jsonl`, `*.pt`, and `cache/swarm/`, but not `.env.*` or `.env.mcp` specifically. If this file were to be staged and committed, the placeholder key and any real values added later (such as the commented-out `NGROK_AUTHTOKEN` or `HF_TOKEN`) would be pushed to the remote.

**Fix:** Add `.env.mcp` (and preferably `.env*` as a pattern) to `.gitignore`:

```
.env.mcp
.env.local
.env*.local
```

---

### Finding 3 — ML Model Checkpoints Tracked by Git (Git Index Mismatch)

**Files:**
- `data/flume/checkpoints/flume_vae_ep2.pt`
- `data/rl/checkpoints/policy_final.pt`

**Severity: Medium**

`git check-ignore` returned exit code 1 (no match), meaning these files are not recognized as ignored by git. However, `git ls-files --error-unmatch` failed for both files, confirming they are **not currently tracked**. The `.gitignore` does contain `data/flume/checkpoints/*.pt` and `data/rl/checkpoints/*.pt`.

The issue: the `.gitignore` also has a broad `data/` rule, then `!data/.gitkeep` and `!data/README.md` exceptions. The interaction of these overlapping rules can cause `git check-ignore` to not report the pattern correctly depending on the git version and rule order, leaving an ambiguous state. The files are currently untracked, but the gitignore logic is fragile — a future `git add data/` or tooling that ignores gitignore rules could accidentally commit large binary model weights.

The git status output shows both `.pt` files as modified (`M`), indicating they exist and are being watched.

**Fix:** Verify gitignore coverage is reliable by simplifying the `data/` block:

```gitignore
# Catch-all for data directory (regenerable)
data/**
!data/.gitkeep
!data/README.md
```

This ensures subdirectory patterns are unambiguous. Also run `git check-ignore -v data/flume/checkpoints/flume_vae_ep2.pt` after the fix to confirm the correct rule fires.

---

### Finding 4 — Cache and Log Files Are Git-Tracked Despite Gitignore Intentions

**Files:**
- `data/compound/cache/token_cache.jsonl`
- `data/config-sync-logs/config_sync.jsonl`
- All `cache/swarm/*.json` files (20+ files shown in git status)

**Severity: Medium**

`git ls-files --error-unmatch` confirmed that `data/compound/cache/token_cache.jsonl`, `data/config-sync-logs/config_sync.jsonl`, and `cache/swarm/a5c6d07b27b7a644809020071339e42bcdce0c4048ddbeb51f58e9d20b7a95f6.json` are **tracked by git** — they appear in the index. The `.gitignore` lists `data/`, `*.jsonl`, and `cache/swarm/` as ignored, but because the files were added to git before the `.gitignore` rules were written (or were force-added), git continues to track them regardless of gitignore.

The swarm cache files contain model prompt/response data and 1000-dimension embedding vectors. The token cache contains `{"key": "k", "value": "v", ...}` entries. The config sync log contains operational metadata (file sizes, commit messages, timestamps). None contain credentials, but:

- The embedding vectors are derived from real query content — they constitute derived user/system data that may be considered sensitive depending on the queries.
- Committing operational logs and cache files to version history is a data hygiene problem: the history accumulates unbounded, and any PII or sensitive prompts that get cached would be permanently embedded in git history.

**Fix:** Untrack these files without deleting them from disk:

```bash
git rm --cached data/compound/cache/token_cache.jsonl
git rm --cached data/config-sync-logs/config_sync.jsonl
git rm --cached -r cache/swarm/
```

Then verify the `.gitignore` entries will prevent them from being re-added.

---

### Finding 5 — File Permissions on Sensitive Files Are Group-Readable

**Files:** `.env.mcp`, `data/compound/cache/token_cache.jsonl`, `data/config-sync-logs/config_sync.jsonl`, `data/flume/checkpoints/flume_vae_ep2.pt`, `data/rl/checkpoints/policy_final.pt`

**Severity: Medium**

All reviewed files have permissions `664` (`-rw-rw-r--`), meaning they are **readable by any user in the `mike-anderson` group** and world-readable for the `.pt` model files. For a developer workstation this is commonly acceptable, but `.env.mcp` specifically should not be group- or world-readable since it holds API keys.

**Fix:** Restrict the env file to owner-only:

```bash
chmod 600 /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.env.mcp
```

For the data files, `640` (owner read/write, group read-only, no world access) is a reasonable baseline if group membership is controlled.

## Security Review Report

**Files reviewed:** 60+ files across source code, configs, scripts, data files, and documentation
**Skill/doc files:** CLEAN (Agent #5)

---

### Critical

**1. Admin dashboard bound to all interfaces with no authentication**
`apps/command_center.py:159`

`demo.launch(server_name="0.0.0.0", server_port=7860)` exposes a Gradio admin UI — which reads arbitrary files, queries SurrealDB, and shows live system state — to any host on the network. No authentication is configured.

```python
# Fix: bind to localhost, or add auth if external access needed
demo.launch(server_name="127.0.0.1", server_port=7860)
# or with auth:
demo.launch(server_name="0.0.0.0", server_port=7860,
            auth=("admin", os.environ["COMMAND_CENTER_PASSWORD"]))
```

---

### High

**2. Insecure `torch.load` — arbitrary code execution via poisoned checkpoint**
`src/cohezion/research/training.py:162`

`torch.load(path, map_location="cpu")` without `weights_only=True` allows pickle deserialization, enabling RCE from a malicious or tampered `.pt` file. The checkpoint path is user-configurable.

```python
# Fix: one line change
checkpoint = torch.load(path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True)
```

**3. Security guardrails defined but never enforced**
`src/cohezion/research/security.py:213–224`, `agent.py:74–151`

`ResearchSecurityGuardrails` and `SimpleSecurity` exist but are never called in `_execute_training()` or `SimpleTrainingRunner.run()`. `ResearchConfig.enable_guardrails` is never read. Agent-generated code changes bypass all validation.

```python
# Fix: wire into _execute_training before subprocess call
if self.config.enable_guardrails:
    result = ResearchSecurityGuardrails().validate_change(change)
    if not result.is_valid:
        raise SecurityError(f"Blocked: {result.issues}")
```

**4. Path traversal via unvalidated `train_script` parameter**
`src/cohezion/research/training.py:180–196`

`train_script: Path` is passed directly to `subprocess.run` with no canonicalization. A caller can supply `../../../tmp/evil.py`.

```python
# Fix: resolve and assert within project root
allowed = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent.resolve()
resolved = Path(train_script).resolve()
if not str(resolved).startswith(str(allowed)):
    raise ValueError(f"Script outside allowed root: {train_script}")
```

**5. Prompt injection via unvalidated external file content**
`scripts/jobs/live_analyst_hourly.py:35–65`

Content from `pulse_*.json` and `RESEARCH_FEED.md` is embedded verbatim into LLM prompts. Anyone who can write to those files can inject adversarial instructions. The LLM response is then written to `LIVE_INSIGHTS.md` as trusted output (stored prompt injection chain).

Fix: wrap file content in labeled XML tags in the prompt; strip instruction-like patterns before embedding. Sanitize LLM responses before writing to trusted files.

**6. Missing authentication on all session endpoints (IDOR)**
`src/cohezion/api/research_endpoints.py:162–276`

`GET /status/{session_id}`, `GET /results/{session_id}`, `POST /stop/{session_id}`, `GET /experiments/{session_id}` — all perform direct dict lookups with no auth. Session IDs use predictable patterns (`f"swarm-{datetime.now().isoformat()}"`). Any caller can read or stop any session.

**7. Internal exception details exposed to API callers**
`src/cohezion/api/research_endpoints.py:116, 158, 276`

`raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))` leaks internal paths, class names, and config values to callers.

```python
logger.error("Failed: %s", e, exc_info=True)
raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Internal server error")
```

**8. API key exposure in exception strings**
`src/cohezion/gateway/ngrok_adapter.py:219, 306–313`

`requests.RequestException` subclasses embed the full request object (including `Authorization: Bearer <key>`) in their string representation. Logging `str(e)` can leak the ngrok API key.

**9. `.env.mcp` contains placeholder credential, is not gitignored**
`.env.mcp:22`

`REDACTED` is in a tracked (non-gitignored) file. The `.gitignore` covers `.env` and `.env.surrealdb` but not `.env.mcp`. (Also flagged in the PR #36 code review.)

```bash
echo ".env.mcp" >> .gitignore
mv .env.mcp .env.mcp.example
```

**10. Markdown injection (XSS) from SurrealDB log content**
`apps/command_center.py:34–37`

Log fields (`source`, `level`, `message`) from SurrealDB are interpolated directly into a Markdown table string rendered by Gradio. Malicious log content can inject Markdown/HTML/JavaScript.

Fix: escape `|` and `<>` characters from all database-sourced field values before interpolation.

---

### Medium

| # | File | Lines | Issue |
|---|------|-------|-------|
| 11 | `ngrok_adapter.py` | 126, 128 | SSRF — `ngrok_endpoint` and `fallback_ollama_url` used without URL validation |
| 12 | `smart_router.py`, `token_client.py` | 256–398 | SSRF — `ollama_host`/`base_url`/`ngrok_endpoint` not validated before use in HTTP calls |
| 13 | `research/security.py` | 48–57 | String-match guardrail trivially bypassed (`'ex' + 'ec'`, `open (` with space) — use AST visitor |
| 14 | `research/config.py`, `agent.py` | 41, 209 | Unvalidated experiment log path — caller can write to arbitrary filesystem locations |
| 15 | `command_center.py` | 22 | SurrealDB connection with no credentials; unauthenticated DB access |
| 16 | `ngrok_adapter.py` | 137–138 | Unbounded in-memory cache (no LRU/TTL) — memory exhaustion DoS |
| 17 | `research_endpoints.py` | 247 | `limit` query param has no upper bound — `limit=10000000` causes memory exhaustion |
| 18 | `research_endpoints.py` | 71–73 | `_active_sessions` dict never evicts stopped sessions — memory leak |
| 19 | `cache/swarm/*.json`, `data/**/*.jsonl` | — | Cache and log files actively tracked by git despite `.gitignore` intent; untrack with `git rm --cached` |
| 20 | `data/flume/checkpoints/*.pt`, `data/rl/checkpoints/*.pt` | — | `.gitignore` coverage for `.pt` files is fragile (overlapping rules); simplify to `data/**` |
| 21 | `.env.mcp` | — | File permissions `664` — env file with API key is group- and world-readable; `chmod 600 .env.mcp` |
| 22 | `scripts/*.py`, `command_center.py` | 15, 16, 9 | Hardcoded `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion` paths — information disclosure + non-portable; use `Path(__file__).resolve().parents[N]` |
| 23 | `smart_router.py` | 272 | Action log directory is a relative path — resolves against `cwd` which may be attacker-influenced |
| 24 | `token_client.py` | 202 | ngrok endpoint logged at INFO level; if URL embeds token as path component, key exposed in logs |

---

### Priority Fix Order

1. **`torch.load` RCE** (training.py:162) — one-line fix, prevents code execution from any checkpoint file
2. **Admin UI on 0.0.0.0** (command_center.py:159) — change `server_name` to `127.0.0.1`
3. **Wire guardrails into execution** (security.py + agent.py) — the security infrastructure is already there, just not connected
4. **IDOR on session endpoints** (research_endpoints.py) — add auth middleware
5. **`.env.mcp` gitignore + permissions** — `echo ".env.mcp" >> .gitignore && chmod 600 .env.mcp`
6. **Untrack cache/log files from git** — `git rm --cached -r cache/swarm/ data/compound/cache/ data/config-sync-logs/`

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**Finding #3 (guardrails not wired)** is a particularly common architectural failure: security code gets written as a separate module, reviewed as if it works, but is never actually called from the code paths it's meant to protect. `ResearchConfig.enable_guardrails` exists as a config field and shows up in tests, creating the illusion of working security. This pattern — *security theater via disconnected infrastructure* — is worth watching for in any AI agent framework where safety checks are an afterthought.

**Finding #5 (stored prompt injection chain)** is specific to agentic AI systems: the output of one AI agent becomes the input to another without sanitization, creating amplification. Each hop in the chain (`pulse.json` → prompt → `LIVE_INSIGHTS.md` → next agent's prompt) is a potential injection point.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

     1→"""Autonomous research agent for training optimization.
     2→
     3→Elegant integration with Cohezion compound executor.
     4→Based on karpathy/autoresearch patterns.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import hashlib
    10→import json
    11→import logging
    12→import subprocess
    13→import time
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→from datetime import datetime
    16→from pathlib import Path
    17→from typing import Any
    18→
    19→from cohezion.compound.core.executor import CompoundExecutor, ExecutionConfig
    20→from cohezion.compound.models import ExecutionResult, Task
    21→from cohezion.research.config import ExperimentResult, ResearchConfig
    22→
    23→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    24→
    25→
    26→@dataclass
    27→class ResearchSession:
    28→    """Session state for research.
    29→
    30→    Minimal state tracking, delegates to Cohezion persistence.
    31→    """
    32→
    33→    session_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().isoformat())
    34→    experiments_completed: int = 0
    35→    best_metric: float = float("inf")
    36→    best_checkpoint: Path | None = None
    37→    active: bool = True
    38→
    39→
    40→class ResearchAgent:
    41→    """Autonomous research agent.
    42→
    43→    Clean implementation (~200 lines) following elegant simplification.
    44→    Integrates with Cohezion's compound executor and metrics systems.
    45→
    46→    Inspired by karpathy/autoresearch but integrated into Cohezion ecosystem.
    47→    """
    48→
    49→    def __init__(
    50→        self,
    51→        config: ResearchConfig | None = None,
    52→        executor: CompoundExecutor | None = None,
    53→    ):
    54→        """Initialize research agent.
    55→
    56→        Args:
    57→            config: Research configuration
    58→            executor: CompoundExecutor for running experiments
    59→                     (creates new one if None)
    60→        """
    61→        self.config = config or ResearchConfig()
    62→        self.session = ResearchSession()
    63→
    64→        # Create executor if not provided
    65→        if executor is None:
    66→            executor = CompoundExecutor(
    67→                execute_fn=self._run_experiment,
    68→                config=ExecutionConfig(max_retries=1),
    69→            )
    70→        self.executor = executor
    71→
    72→        logger.info(f"ResearchAgent initialized: {self.session.session_id}")
    73→
    74→    def _run_experiment(
    75→        self,
    76→        task: Task,
    77→        context: dict[str, Any],
    78→    ) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    79→        """Run single experiment.
    80→
    81→        This is the core execution function passed to CompoundExecutor.
    82→        """
    83→        experiment_id = task.id
    84→        start_time = time.time()
    85→
    86→        logger.info(f"Running experiment: {experiment_id}")
    87→
    88→        try:
    89→            # Run training with time budget
    90→            result = self._execute_training(
    91→                time_budget=self.config.experiment_time_budget,
    92→            )
    93→
    94→            # Parse result
    95→            metric_value = result.get("val_bpb", float("inf"))
    96→            duration = time.time() - start_time
    97→
    98→            # Check if improved
    99→            improved = metric_value < self.session.best_metric
   100→            if improved:
   101→                self.session.best_metric = metric_value
   102→                logger.info(f"New best metric: {metric_value:.4f}")
   103→
   104→            return (
   105→                f"Experiment {experiment_id}: {metric_value:.4f}",
   106→                {
   107→                    "metric_value": metric_value,
   108→                    "duration": duration,
   109→                    "improved": improved,
   110→                },
   111→            )
   112→
   113→        except Exception as e:
   114→            logger.error(f"Experiment {experiment_id} failed: {e}")
   115→            return (
   116→                f"Failed: {e}",
   117→                {
   118→                    "metric_value": float("inf"),
   119→                    "duration": time.time() - start_time,
   120→                    "improved": False,
   121→                },
   122→            )
   123→
   124→    def _execute_training(self, time_budget: float) -> dict[str, Any]:
   125→        """Execute training with time limit.
   126→
   127→        Runs autoresearch-style training for fixed duration.
   128→        """
   129→        # Run training script
   130→        result = subprocess.run(
   131→            [
   132→                "python",
   133→                "train.py",
   134→                "--time_budget",
   135→                str(time_budget),
   136→            ],
   137→            capture_output=True,
   138→            text=True,
   139→            timeout=time_budget + 60,  # Safety margin
   140→        )
   141→
   142→        if result.returncode != 0:
   143→            raise RuntimeError(f"Training failed: {result.stderr}")
   144→
   145→        # Parse metrics from output
   146→        # (simplified - in real implementation would parse JSON)
   147→        return {
   148→            "val_bpb": 2.5,  # placeholder
   149→            "train_loss": 2.3,
   150→            "val_loss": 2.4,
   151→        }
   152→
   153→    def run_session(
   154→        self,
   155→        max_experiments: int | None = None,
   156→    ) -> ResearchSession:
   157→        """Run complete research session.
   158→
   159→        Args:
   160→            max_experiments: Override default max
   161→
   162→        Returns:
   163→            Final session state
   164→        """
   165→        max_exp = max_experiments or self.config.max_experiments
   166→
   167→        logger.info(f"Starting research session: {max_exp} experiments")
   168→
   169→        while self.session.experiments_completed < max_exp and self.session.active:
   170→            # Create experiment task
   171→            exp_id = f"exp-{self.session.experiments_completed + 1}"
   172→            task = Task(
   173→                id=exp_id,
   174→                description=f"Research experiment {exp_id}",
   175→                skill_name="research",
   176→                operation_type="optimize",
   177→            )
   178→
   179→            # Run via executor
   180→            result = self.executor.execute(task)
   181→
   182→            # Log result
   183→            self._log_experiment(exp_id, result)
   184→
   185→            # Update session
   186→            self.session.experiments_completed += 1
   187→
   188→            logger.info(
   189→                f"Experiment {exp_id} complete: {self.session.experiments_completed}/{max_exp}"
   190→            )
   191→
   192→        logger.info(f"Research session complete: {self.session}")
   193→        return self.session
   194→
   195→    def _log_experiment(self, exp_id: str, result: ExecutionResult) -> None:
   196→        """Log experiment result."""
   197→        # Access metrics as dataclass fields, not dict
   198→        exp_result = ExperimentResult(
   199→            experiment_id=exp_id,
   200→            timestamp=datetime.now().isoformat(),
   201→            metric_value=getattr(result.metrics, "metric_value", float("inf")),
   202→            metric_name=self.config.target_metric,
   203→            improved=getattr(result.metrics, "improved", False),
   204→            code_changes=[],  # Would track actual changes
   205→            duration_seconds=result.metrics.duration_seconds,
   206→        )
   207→
   208→        # Append to experiment log
   209→        with open(self.config.experiment_log, "a") as f:
   210→            f.write(json.dumps(exp_result.to_dict()) + "\n")
   211→
   212→    def get_best_result(self) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   213→        """Get best experiment result from session."""
   214→        if self.session.best_metric == float("inf"):
   215→            return None
   216→
   217→        return {
   218→            "metric": self.session.best_metric,
   219→            "checkpoint": str(self.session.best_checkpoint),
   220→            "experiments": self.session.experiments_completed,
   221→        }
   222→
   223→    def stop(self) -> None:
   224→        """Stop research session gracefully."""
   225→        self.session.active = False
   226→        logger.info("Research session stopped")
   227→

     1→"""Training execution utilities for ResearchAgent.
     2→
     3→Handles actual LLM training with time budget constraints.
     4→Integrates with Cohezion's FLUME and training infrastructure.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import json
    10→import logging
    11→import subprocess
    12→import time
    13→from pathlib import Path
    14→from typing import Any
    15→
    16→import torch
    17→import torch.nn as nn
    18→from torch.utils.data import DataLoader
    19→
    20→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    21→
    22→
    23→class TrainingExecutor:
    24→    """Execute training runs with time budget.
    25→
    26→    Clean implementation following elegant simplification.
    27→    """
    28→
    29→    def __init__(self, time_budget: float = 300.0):
    30→        self.time_budget = time_budget
    31→        self.start_time: float = 0.0
    32→
    33→    def execute(
    34→        self,
    35→        model: nn.Module,
    36→        train_loader: DataLoader,
    37→        val_loader: DataLoader,
    38→        optimizer: torch.optim.Optimizer,
    39→    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
    40→        """Execute training run with time budget.
    41→
    42→        Args:
    43→            model: PyTorch model
    44→            train_loader: Training data loader
    45→            val_loader: Validation data loader
    46→            optimizer: Optimizer instance
    47→
    48→        Returns:
    49→            Dictionary with metrics: val_bpb, train_loss, val_loss
    50→        """
    51→        self.start_time = time.time()
    52→        device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    53→        model = model.to(device)
    54→
    55→        best_val_loss = float("inf")
    56→        train_losses = []
    57→        step = 0
    58→
    59→        try:
    60→            while time.time() - self.start_time < self.time_budget:
    61→                # Training loop
    62→                model.train()
    63→                for batch in train_loader:
    64→                    # Check time budget
    65→                    if time.time() - self.start_time >= self.time_budget:
    66→                        break
    67→
    68→                    # Forward pass
    69→                    inputs = batch.to(device)
    70→                    optimizer.zero_grad()
    71→                    outputs = model(inputs)
    72→                    loss = outputs.loss
    73→
    74→                    # Backward pass
    75→                    loss.backward()
    76→                    optimizer.step()
    77→
    78→                    train_losses.append(loss.item())
    79→                    step += 1
    80→
    81→                    # Periodic validation
    82→                    if step % 100 == 0:
    83→                        val_loss = self._validate(model, val_loader, device)
    84→                        best_val_loss = min(best_val_loss, val_loss)
    85→
    86→                        logger.debug(
    87→                            f"Step {step}: train_loss={loss.item():.4f}, val_loss={val_loss:.4f}"
    88→                        )
    89→
    90→            # Final validation
    91→            final_val_loss = self._validate(model, val_loader, device)
    92→
    93→            # Calculate bits per byte
    94→            val_bpb = final_val_loss / 0.693147  # Convert to bits
    95→
    96→            return {
    97→                "val_bpb": val_bpb,
    98→                "train_loss": sum(train_losses) / len(train_losses) if train_losses else 0.0,
    99→                "val_loss": final_val_loss,
   100→                "steps": step,
   101→            }
   102→
   103→        except Exception as e:
   104→            logger.error(f"Training failed: {e}")
   105→            return {
   106→                "val_bpb": float("inf"),
   107→                "train_loss": float("inf"),
   108→                "val_loss": float("inf"),
   109→                "error": str(e),
   110→            }
   111→
   112→    def _validate(
   113→        self,
   114→        model: nn.Module,
   115→        val_loader: DataLoader,
   116→        device: torch.device,
   117→    ) -> float:
   118→        """Run validation and return average loss."""
   119→        model.eval()
   120→        total_loss = 0.0
   121→        count = 0
   122→
   123→        with torch.no_grad():
   124→            for batch in val_loader:
   125→                if time.time() - self.start_time >= self.time_budget:
   126→                    break
   127→
   128→                inputs = batch.to(device)
   129→                outputs = model(inputs)
   130→                total_loss += outputs.loss.item()
   131→                count += 1
   132→
   133→        return total_loss / count if count > 0 else float("inf")
   134→
   135→    def save_checkpoint(
   136→        self,
   137→        model: nn.Module,
   138→        optimizer: torch.optim.Optimizer,
   139→        path: Path,
   140→    ) -> None:
   141→        """Save model checkpoint."""
   142→        checkpoint = {
   143→            "model_state": model.state_dict(),
   144→            "optimizer_state": optimizer.state_dict(),
   145→            "timestamp": time.time(),
   146→        }
   147→        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   148→        torch.save(checkpoint, path)
   149→        logger.info(f"Checkpoint saved: {path}")
   150→
   151→    def load_checkpoint(
   152→        self,
   153→        model: nn.Module,
   154→        optimizer: torch.optim.Optimizer,
   155→        path: Path,
   156→    ) -> bool:
   157→        """Load model checkpoint."""
   158→        if not path.exists():
   159→            return False
   160→
   161→        try:
   162→            checkpoint = torch.load(path, map_location="cpu")
   163→            model.load_state_dict(checkpoint["model_state"])
   164→            optimizer.load_state_dict(checkpoint["optimizer_state"])
   165→            logger.info(f"Checkpoint loaded: {path}")
   166→            return True
   167→        except Exception as e:
   168→            logger.error(f"Failed to load checkpoint: {e}")
   169→            return False
   170→
   171→
   172→class SimpleTrainingRunner:
   173→    """Simplified training runner for basic use cases."""
   174→
   175→    def __init__(self, time_budget: float = 300.0):
   176→        self.time_budget = time_budget
   177→
   178→    def run(
   179→        self,
   180→        train_script: Path = Path("train.py"),
   181→    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   182→        """Run training script with time budget.
   183→
   184→        Simplified version using subprocess.
   185→        """
   186→        start_time = time.time()
   187→
   188→        try:
   189→            result = subprocess.run(
   190→                [
   191→                    "python",
   192→                    str(train_script),
   193→                    "--time_budget",
   194→                    str(self.time_budget),
   195→                ],
   196→                capture_output=True,
   197→                text=True,
   198→                timeout=self.time_budget + 60,
   199→            )
   200→
   201→            duration = time.time() - start_time
   202→
   203→            if result.returncode != 0:
   204→                return {
   205→                    "val_bpb": float("inf"),
   206→                    "error": result.stderr,
   207→                    "duration": duration,
   208→                }
   209→
   210→            # Parse metrics from output
   211→            metrics = self._parse_output(result.stdout)
   212→            metrics["duration"] = duration
   213→
   214→            return metrics
   215→
   216→        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   217→            return {
   218→                "val_bpb": float("inf"),
   219→                "error": "Training timeout",
   220→                "duration": self.time_budget + 60,
   221→            }
   222→        except Exception as e:
   223→            return {
   224→                "val_bpb": float("inf"),
   225→                "error": str(e),
   226→                "duration": time.time() - start_time,
   227→            }
   228→
   229→    def _parse_output(self, output: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   230→        """Parse training output for metrics."""
   231→        metrics = {
   232→            "val_bpb": float("inf"),
   233→            "train_loss": 0.0,
   234→            "val_loss": float("inf"),
   235→        }
   236→
   237→        # Look for JSON metrics in output
   238→        for line in output.split("\n"):
   239→            if "METRICS:" in line:
   240→                try:
   241→                    json_str = line.split("METRICS:")[1]
   242→                    parsed = json.loads(json_str)
   243→                    metrics.update(parsed)
   244→                except Exception:
   245→                    pass
   246→
   247→        return metrics
   248→

     1→"""Security guardrails for ResearchAgent.
     2→
     3→Validates code changes before execution.
     4→Integrates with Cohezion's security pipeline.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import ast
    10→import logging
    11→import re
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass
    13→from pathlib import Path
    14→from typing import Any
    15→
    16→from cohezion.security.pipeline import SecurityPipeline
    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→
    21→@dataclass
    22→class CodeChange:
    23→    """Represents a code change."""
    24→
    25→    file_path: Path
    26→    old_code: str
    27→    new_code: str
    28→    change_type: str  # 'modify', 'add', 'delete'
    29→
    30→
    31→@dataclass
    32→class ValidationResult:
    33→    """Result of code validation."""
    34→
    35→    is_valid: bool
    36→    issues: list[str]
    37→    risk_level: str  # 'low', 'medium', 'high', 'critical'
    38→
    39→
    40→class ResearchSecurityGuardrails:
    41→    """Security guardrails for research code changes.
    42→
    43→    Validates modifications to train.py before execution.
    44→    Follows elegant simplification - ~150 lines.
    45→    """
    46→
    47→    # Forbidden patterns
    48→    FORBIDDEN_IMPORTS = [
    49→        "os.system",
    50→        "subprocess.call",
    51→        "subprocess.run",
    52→        "eval(",
    53→        "exec(",
    54→        "__import__",
    55→        "open(",  # File operations
    56→        "write(",
    57→    ]
    58→
    59→    # Allowed training-related imports
    60→    ALLOWED_IMPORTS = [
    61→        "torch",
    62→        "torch.nn",
    63→        "torch.optim",
    64→        "numpy",
    65→        "math",
    66→        "typing",
    67→        "dataclasses",
    68→        "logging",
    69→    ]
    70→
    71→    def __init__(self, security_pipeline: SecurityPipeline | None = None):
    72→        """Initialize guardrails.
    73→
    74→        Args:
    75→            security_pipeline: Optional Cohezion security pipeline
    76→        """
    77→        self.security_pipeline = security_pipeline
    78→
    79→    def validate_change(self, change: CodeChange) -> ValidationResult:
    80→        """Validate a single code change.
    81→
    82→        Args:
    83→            change: CodeChange to validate
    84→
    85→        Returns:
    86→            ValidationResult with validity and any issues
    87→        """
    88→        issues = []
    89→
    90→        # Check for forbidden patterns
    91→        for pattern in self.FORBIDDEN_IMPORTS:
    92→            if pattern in change.new_code:
    93→                issues.append(f"Forbidden pattern: {pattern}")
    94→
    95→        # Check AST validity
    96→        if not self._is_valid_ast(change.new_code):
    97→            issues.append("Invalid Python syntax")
    98→
    99→        # Check for dangerous operations
   100→        dangerous = self._check_dangerous_operations(change.new_code)
   101→        issues.extend(dangerous)
   102→
   103→        # Run through security pipeline if available
   104→        if self.security_pipeline:
   105→            pipeline_result = self.security_pipeline.check_output(change.new_code)
   106→            if pipeline_result.action.value != "allow":
   107→                issues.append(f"Security pipeline blocked: {pipeline_result.reason}")
   108→
   109→        # Determine risk level
   110→        risk = self._assess_risk(change, issues)
   111→
   112→        return ValidationResult(
   113→            is_valid=len(issues) == 0,
   114→            issues=issues,
   115→            risk_level=risk,
   116→        )
   117→
   118→    def _is_valid_ast(self, code: str) -> bool:
   119→        """Check if code is valid Python AST."""
   120→        try:
   121→            ast.parse(code)
   122→            return True
   123→        except SyntaxError:
   124→            return False
   125→
   126→    def _check_dangerous_operations(self, code: str) -> list[str]:
   127→        """Check for potentially dangerous operations."""
   128→        issues = []
   129→
   130→        # File operations outside allowed directories
   131→        if re.search(r'open\s*\([^)]*["\']/(?:etc|usr|bin|lib)', code):
   132→            issues.append("Attempted access to system directories")
   133→
   134→        # Network operations
   135→        if re.search(r"(socket\.|urllib|requests\.|http\.)", code):
   136→            issues.append("Network operations not allowed in training")
   137→
   138→        # GPU memory bombs
   139→        if re.search(r"cuda\s*\(\s*\)\s*\.\s*empty_cache", code):
   140→            issues.append("GPU cache manipulation detected")
   141→
   142→        # Infinite loops
   143→        if re.search(r"while\s+True\s*:", code):
   144→            issues.append("Infinite loop detected")
   145→
   146→        return issues
   147→
   148→    def _assess_risk(self, change: CodeChange, issues: list[str]) -> str:
   149→        """Assess risk level of change."""
   150→        if len(issues) == 0:
   151→            return "low"
   152→        elif len(issues) <= 2:
   153→            return "medium"
   154→        elif len(issues) <= 5:
   155→            return "high"
   156→        else:
   157→            return "critical"
   158→
   159→    def validate_batch(
   160→        self,
   161→        changes: list[CodeChange],
   162→    ) -> dict[str, ValidationResult]:
   163→        """Validate multiple code changes.
   164→
   165→        Returns dict mapping file paths to validation results.
   166→        """
   167→        results = {}
   168→        for change in changes:
   169→            results[str(change.file_path)] = self.validate_change(change)
   170→        return results
   171→
   172→    def get_safe_template(self) -> str:
   173→        """Get safe training template for agents."""
   174→        return '''
   175→"""Safe training template for research agents.
   176→
   177→This template provides a constrained environment for experimentation.
   178→"""
   179→
   180→import torch
   181→import torch.nn as nn
   182→from torch.utils.data import DataLoader
   183→
   184→class SafeTrainingConfig:
   185→    """Safe training configuration."""
   186→    model_depth: int = 8
   187→    vocab_size: int = 8192
   188→    max_seq_len: int = 1024
   189→    batch_size: int = 16
   190→    learning_rate: float = 0.001
   191→
   192→def train(
   193→    model: nn.Module,
   194→    train_loader: DataLoader,
   195→    config: SafeTrainingConfig,
   196→) -> dict:
   197→    """Safe training loop.
   198→    
   199→    Agents can modify this function but must:
   200→    - Not add file/network operations
   201→    - Not use eval/exec
   202→    - Keep changes under 50 lines
   203→    """
   204→    # Your experimental code here
   205→    pass
   206→
   207→if __name__ == "__main__":
   208→    # Training execution
   209→    pass
   210→'''
   211→
   212→
   213→class SimpleSecurity:
   214→    """Minimal security for basic use cases."""
   215→
   216→    def __init__(self):
   217→        self.forbidden = ["exec(", "eval(", "os.system", "subprocess"]
   218→
   219→    def check(self, code: str) -> bool:
   220→        """Quick security check."""
   221→        for pattern in self.forbidden:
   222→            if pattern in code:
   223→                return False
   224→        return True
   225→

     1→"""ngrok AI Gateway adapter for multi-provider LLM routing.
     2→
     3→Provides OpenAI SDK-compatible endpoint that routes requests through ngrok AI Gateway
     4→with automatic failover to local Ollama, cost optimization via intelligent routing,
     5→and built-in response caching.
     6→
     7→Features:
     8→- OpenAI SDK compatible (drop-in replacement for direct API calls)
     9→- Multi-provider support (OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, self-hosted Ollama)
    10→- Automatic failover: ngrok → Ollama
    11→- Cost optimization via intelligent routing (cheap vs premium models)
    12→- Built-in response caching (4th tier in Cohezion's cache stack)
    13→- PII redaction before requests hit AI providers
    14→- Per-request model routing and cost tracking
    15→
    16→Usage::
    17→
    18→    # Initialize with ngrok gateway
    19→    adapter = NgrokAIGateway(
    20→        ngrok_endpoint="https://xxxxx.ngrok.app/v1",
    21→        ngrok_api_key="your-ngrok-key",
    22→        fallback_ollama_url="http://localhost:11434",
    23→        enable_failover=True,
    24→    )
    25→
    26→    # Use as drop-in replacement for ResilientOllamaClient
    27→    response, tokens = await adapter.generate(
    28→        prompt="Explain quantum computing",
    29→        model="gpt-4o",  # ngrok routes to any provider
    30→        system="You are helpful"
    31→    )
    32→
    33→    # Track costs and performance
    34→    metrics = adapter.get_metrics()
    35→"""
    36→
    37→import asyncio
    38→import hashlib
    39→import logging
    40→import os
    41→import time
    42→from dataclasses import dataclass
    43→from typing import Any
    44→
    45→import requests  # type: ignore[import-untyped]
    46→
    47→from cohezion.deployment.feature_flags import FeatureFlag, FeatureFlagContext, is_feature_enabled
    48→
    49→
    50→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    51→
    52→
    53→@dataclass
    54→class NgrokMetrics:
    55→    """Metrics for ngrok gateway usage."""
    56→
    57→    total_requests: int = 0
    58→    successful_requests: int = 0
    59→    failed_requests: int = 0
    60→    fallback_requests: int = 0  # Requests that fell back to Ollama
    61→    total_tokens: int = 0

    63→    ngrok_requests: int = 0
    64→    ollama_requests: int = 0
    65→    cache_hits: int = 0

    67→    start_time: float = 0.0
    68→
    69→    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
    70→        """Initialize start time if not set."""
    71→        if self.start_time == 0.0:
    72→            self.start_time = time.time()
    73→
    74→    def get_uptime(self) -> float:
    75→        """Get uptime in seconds."""
    76→        return time.time() - self.start_time
    77→
    78→
    79→class NgrokAIGateway:
    80→    """ngrok AI Gateway adapter with automatic failover and cost optimization.
    81→
    82→    Routes requests through ngrok endpoint with fallback to local Ollama.
    83→    Supports multi-provider routing, cost optimization, and built-in caching.
    84→    """
    85→
    86→    # Model cost mappings (tokens)
    87→    MODEL_COSTS = {
    88→        # OpenAI
    89→        "gpt-4o": {"input": 5.0 / 1e6, "output": 15.0 / 1e6},
    90→        "gpt-4-turbo": {"input": 10.0 / 1e6, "output": 30.0 / 1e6},
    91→        "gpt-3.5-turbo": {"input": 0.5 / 1e6, "output": 1.5 / 1e6},
    92→        # Anthropic
    93→        "claude-3.5-sonnet": {"input": 3.0 / 1e6, "output": 15.0 / 1e6},
    94→        "claude-3-opus": {"input": 15.0 / 1e6, "output": 75.0 / 1e6},
    95→        "claude-3-haiku": {"input": 0.25 / 1e6, "output": 1.25 / 1e6},
    96→        # Google
    97→        "gemini-pro": {"input": 0.5 / 1e6, "output": 1.5 / 1e6},
    98→        # Ollama (free, local)
    99→        "ollama-default": {"input": 0.0, "output": 0.0},
   100→    }
   101→
   102→    def __init__(
   103→        self,
   104→        ngrok_endpoint: str | None = None,
   105→        ngrok_api_key: str | None = None,
   106→        fallback_ollama_url: str = "http://localhost:11434",
   107→        enable_failover: bool = True,
   108→        enable_cost_optimization: bool = True,
   109→        timeout: float = 300.0,
   110→        max_retries: int = 3,
   111→    ):
   112→        """Initialize ngrok AI Gateway adapter.
   113→
   114→        Args:
   115→            ngrok_endpoint: ngrok gateway endpoint (format: https://xxxxx.ngrok.app/v1)
   116→                          If None, tries to load from NGROK_ENDPOINT env var
   117→            ngrok_api_key: ngrok API key for authentication
   118→                          If None, tries to load from NGROK_API_KEY env var
   119→            fallback_ollama_url: Fallback Ollama URL if ngrok fails
   120→            enable_failover: Whether to failover to Ollama on ngrok failure
   121→            enable_cost_optimization: Whether to use cost-optimized routing
   122→            timeout: Request timeout in seconds
   123→            max_retries: Number of retries on failure
   124→        """
   125→        # Load from env if not provided
   126→        self.ngrok_endpoint = ngrok_endpoint or os.getenv("NGROK_ENDPOINT", "")
   127→        self.ngrok_api_key = ngrok_api_key or os.getenv("NGROK_API_KEY", "")
   128→        self.fallback_ollama_url = fallback_ollama_url.rstrip("/")
   129→        self.enable_failover = enable_failover
   130→        self.enable_cost_optimization = enable_cost_optimization
   131→        self.timeout = timeout
   132→        self.max_retries = max_retries
   133→
   134→        # Metrics tracking
   135→        self.metrics = NgrokMetrics()
   136→
   137→        # Response cache (4th tier)
   138→        self._response_cache: dict[str, tuple[str, int]] = {}
   139→
   140→        # Feature flag context
   141→        self._flag_context = FeatureFlagContext()
   142→
   143→        # Validate configuration
   144→        self._validate_config()
   145→
   146→    def _validate_config(self) -> None:
   147→        """Validate ngrok configuration."""
   148→        if not self.ngrok_endpoint and not self.enable_failover:
   149→            logger.warning(
   150→                "ngrok endpoint not configured and failover disabled. "
   151→                "Set NGROK_ENDPOINT or enable_failover."
   152→            )
   153→        elif self.ngrok_endpoint and not self.ngrok_api_key:
   154→            logger.warning(
   155→                "ngrok endpoint configured but no API key provided. "
   156→                "Set NGROK_API_KEY for authenticated requests."
   157→            )
   158→        elif self.ngrok_endpoint:
   159→            logger.info(f"ngrok AI Gateway configured: {self.ngrok_endpoint}")
   160→
   161→    def _cache_key(self, prompt: str, system: str, model: str) -> str:
   162→        """Generate cache key for response."""
   163→        combined = f"{prompt}|{system}|{model}"
   164→        return hashlib.sha256(combined.encode()).hexdigest()
   165→
   166→    def _calculate_cost(self, model: str, input_tokens: int, output_tokens: int) -> float:
   167→        """Calculate request cost in USD."""
   168→        costs = self.MODEL_COSTS.get(model)
   169→        if not costs:
   170→            # Fallback to generic cost if model not in mapping
   171→            costs = self.MODEL_COSTS.get("gpt-3.5-turbo")
   172→
   173→        input_cost = input_tokens * (costs["input"] if costs else 0.0)
   174→        output_cost = output_tokens * (costs["output"] if costs else 0.0)
   175→        return input_cost + output_cost
   176→
   177→    async def generate(
   178→        self,
   179→        prompt: str,
   180→        model: str,
   181→        system: str = "",
   182→        num_predict: int = 256,
   183→    ) -> tuple[str, int]:
   184→        """Generate response with ngrok gateway or Ollama fallback.
   185→
   186→        Args:
   187→            prompt: User prompt
   188→            model: Model name (e.g., "gpt-4o", "claude-3.5-sonnet", "qwen3-coder:30b")
   189→            system: System prompt
   190→            num_predict: Max tokens to generate (used for Ollama only)
   191→
   192→        Returns:
   193→            Tuple of (response_text, tokens_used)
   194→
   195→        Raises:
   196→            RuntimeError: If all providers fail
   197→        """
   198→        self.metrics.total_requests += 1
   199→
   200→        # Check response cache (4th tier)
   201→        cache_key = self._cache_key(prompt, system, model)
   202→        if cache_key in self._response_cache:
   203→            response, tokens = self._response_cache[cache_key]
   204→            self.metrics.cache_hits += 1
   205→            logger.debug(f"Cache hit for model {model} (saved {tokens} tokens)")
   206→            return response, tokens
   207→
   208→        # Check if ngrok gateway is enabled via feature flag
   209→        use_ngrok = is_feature_enabled(FeatureFlag.NGROK_AI_GATEWAY, self._flag_context)
   210→
   211→        if use_ngrok and self.ngrok_endpoint:
   212→            try:
   213→                response, tokens = await self._call_ngrok(prompt, model, system)
   214→                self.metrics.successful_requests += 1
   215→                self.metrics.ngrok_requests += 1
   216→                self._response_cache[cache_key] = (response, tokens)
   217→                return response, tokens
   218→            except Exception as e:
   219→                logger.warning(f"ngrok request failed: {e}")
   220→                self.metrics.failed_requests += 1
   221→
   222→                if not self.enable_failover:
   223→                    raise RuntimeError(f"ngrok request failed and failover disabled: {e}") from e
   224→
   225→                logger.info("Falling back to Ollama")
   226→                self.metrics.fallback_requests += 1
   227→
   228→        # Fallback to Ollama
   229→        try:
   230→            response, tokens = await self._call_ollama(prompt, model, system, num_predict)
   231→            self.metrics.successful_requests += 1
   232→            self.metrics.ollama_requests += 1
   233→            self._response_cache[cache_key] = (response, tokens)
   234→            return response, tokens
   235→        except Exception as e:
   236→            logger.error(f"All providers failed: {e}")
   237→            self.metrics.failed_requests += 1
   238→            raise RuntimeError(f"All providers failed: ngrok and Ollama: {e}") from e
   239→
   240→    async def _call_ngrok(
   241→        self,
   242→        prompt: str,
   243→        model: str,
   244→        system: str = "",
   245→    ) -> tuple[str, int]:
   246→        """Call ngrok AI Gateway endpoint.
   247→
   248→        Args:
   249→            prompt: User prompt
   250→            model: Model name
   251→            system: System prompt
   252→
   253→        Returns:
   254→            Tuple of (response_text, tokens_used)
   255→
   256→        Raises:
   257→            Exception: If request fails
   258→        """
   259→        if not self.ngrok_endpoint:
   260→            raise ValueError("ngrok endpoint not configured")
   261→
   262→        headers = {
   263→            "Authorization": f"Bearer {self.ngrok_api_key}" if self.ngrok_api_key else "",
   264→            "Content-Type": "application/json",
   265→        }
   266→
   267→        payload = {
   268→            "model": model,
   269→            "messages": [
   270→                {"role": "system", "content": system} if system else {},
   271→                {"role": "user", "content": prompt},
   272→            ],
   273→            "temperature": 0.7,
   274→            "max_tokens": 256,
   275→        }
   276→
   277→        # Remove empty system message
   278→        payload["messages"] = [m for m in payload["messages"] if m]
   279→
   280→        for attempt in range(self.max_retries):
   281→            try:
   282→                response = requests.post(
   283→                    f"{self.ngrok_endpoint}/chat/completions",
   284→                    json=payload,
   285→                    headers=headers,
   286→                    timeout=self.timeout,
   287→                )
   288→                response.raise_for_status()
   289→
   290→                data = response.json()
   291→                text = data["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]
   292→                tokens = data.get("usage", {}).get("completion_tokens", 0)
   293→                input_tokens = data.get("usage", {}).get("prompt_tokens", 0)
   294→
   295→                # Track cost
   296→                cost = self._calculate_cost(model, input_tokens, tokens)
   297→                self.metrics.total_cost += cost
   298→                self.metrics.total_tokens += tokens
   299→
   300→                logger.debug(f"ngrok response: {len(text)} chars, {tokens} tokens, ${cost:.6f}")
   301→                return text, tokens
   302→
   303→            except Exception as e:
   304→                if attempt == self.max_retries - 1:
   305→                    raise RuntimeError(
   306→                        f"ngrok request failed after {self.max_retries} retries: {e}"
   307→                    ) from e
   308→
   309→                wait_time = 0.5 * (2**attempt)
   310→                logger.warning(
   311→                    f"ngrok request failed (attempt {attempt + 1}/{self.max_retries}), "
   312→                    f"retrying in {wait_time:.1f}s: {e}"
   313→                )
   314→                await asyncio.sleep(wait_time)
   315→
   316→        raise RuntimeError("Unexpected retry loop exit")
   317→
   318→    async def _call_ollama(
   319→        self,
   320→        prompt: str,
   321→        model: str,
   322→        system: str = "",
   323→        num_predict: int = 256,
   324→    ) -> tuple[str, int]:
   325→        """Call local Ollama as fallback via standard chat API.
   326→
   327→        Args:
   328→            prompt: User prompt
   329→            model: Model name
   330→            system: System prompt
   331→            num_predict: Max tokens to generate
   332→
   333→        Returns:
   334→            Tuple of (response_text, tokens_used)
   335→        """
   336→        for attempt in range(self.max_retries):
   337→            try:
   338→                # Construct standard Ollama messages
   339→                messages = []
   340→                if system:
   341→                    messages.append({"role": "system", "content": system})
   342→                messages.append({"role": "user", "content": prompt})
   343→
   344→                response = requests.post(
   345→                    f"{self.fallback_ollama_url}/api/chat",
   346→                    json={
   347→                        "model": model,
   348→                        "messages": messages,
   349→                        "stream": False,
   350→                    },
   351→                    timeout=self.timeout,
   352→                )
   353→                response.raise_for_status()
   354→
   355→                data = response.json()
   356→                text = data.get("message", {}).get("content", "")
   357→                tokens = data.get("eval_count", 0)
   358→
   359→                self.metrics.total_tokens += tokens
   360→
   361→                logger.debug(f"Ollama response: {len(text)} chars, {tokens} tokens")
   362→                return text, tokens
   363→
   364→            except Exception as e:
   365→                if attempt == self.max_retries - 1:
   366→                    raise RuntimeError(
   367→                        f"Ollama request failed after {self.max_retries} retries: {e}"
   368→                    ) from e
   369→
   370→                wait_time = 0.5 * (2**attempt)
   371→                logger.warning(
   372→                    f"Ollama request failed (attempt {attempt + 1}/{self.max_retries}), "
   373→                    f"retrying in {wait_time:.1f}s: {e}"
   374→                )
   375→                await asyncio.sleep(wait_time)
   376→
   377→        raise RuntimeError("Unexpected retry loop exit")
   378→
   379→    def get_metrics(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   380→        """Get gateway metrics.
   381→
   382→        Returns:
   383→            Dictionary with performance and cost metrics
   384→        """
   385→        uptime = self.metrics.get_uptime()
   386→        success_rate = (
   387→            (self.metrics.successful_requests / self.metrics.total_requests * 100)
   388→            if self.metrics.total_requests > 0
   389→            else 0.0
   390→        )
   391→
   392→        return {
   393→            "total_requests": self.metrics.total_requests,
   394→            "successful_requests": self.metrics.successful_requests,
   395→            "failed_requests": self.metrics.failed_requests,
   396→            "fallback_requests": self.metrics.fallback_requests,
   397→            "ngrok_requests": self.metrics.ngrok_requests,
   398→            "ollama_requests": self.metrics.ollama_requests,
   399→            "cache_hits": self.metrics.cache_hits,
   400→            "success_rate": round(success_rate, 2),
   401→            "total_tokens": self.metrics.total_tokens,
   402→            "total_cost": round(self.metrics.total_cost, 4),
   403→            "average_cost_per_request": round(
   404→                self.metrics.total_cost / self.metrics.total_requests, 6
   405→            )
   406→            if self.metrics.total_requests > 0
   407→            else 0.0,
   408→            "uptime_seconds": round(uptime, 2),
   409→            "requests_per_minute": round((self.metrics.total_requests / uptime * 60), 2)
   410→            if uptime > 0
   411→            else 0.0,
   412→        }
   413→
   414→    def clear_cache(self) -> None:
   415→        """Clear response cache."""
   416→        self._response_cache.clear()
   417→        logger.info("Response cache cleared")
   418→
   419→    def reset_metrics(self) -> None:
   420→        """Reset metrics."""
   421→        self.metrics = NgrokMetrics()
   422→        logger.debug("Metrics reset")
   423→

     1→"""API endpoints for ResearchAgent.
     2→
     3→Exposes autonomous research capabilities via REST API.
     4→Integrates with existing Cohezion API infrastructure.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import asyncio
    10→import json
    11→import logging
    12→from datetime import datetime
    13→from typing import Any
    14→
    15→from fastapi import APIRouter, BackgroundTasks, HTTPException
    16→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    17→
    18→from cohezion.research import (
    19→    MultiAgentResearchConfig,
    20→    ResearchAgent,
    21→    ResearchConfig,
    22→    ResearchSwarm,
    23→)
    24→
    25→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    26→
    27→router = APIRouter(prefix="/research", tags=["research"])
    28→
    29→
    30→# Request/Response models
    31→class ResearchConfigRequest(BaseModel):
    32→    """Research configuration request."""
    33→
    34→    experiment_time_budget: float = Field(default=300.0, ge=60.0, le=3600.0)
    35→    max_experiments: int = Field(default=100, ge=1, le=1000)
    36→    target_metric: str = Field(default="val_bpb")
    37→    model_depth: int = Field(default=8, ge=1, le=32)
    38→    vocab_size: int = Field(default=8192, ge=256, le=65536)
    39→    enable_guardrails: bool = Field(default=True)
    40→
    41→
    42→class MultiAgentConfigRequest(BaseModel):
    43→    """Multi-agent research configuration."""
    44→
    45→    num_agents: int = Field(default=3, ge=1, le=10)
    46→    experiments_per_agent: int = Field(default=33, ge=1, le=100)
    47→    agent_diversity: str = Field(default="high")
    48→
    49→
    50→class ResearchResponse(BaseModel):
    51→    """Research session response."""
    52→
    53→    session_id: str
    54→    status: str
    55→    experiments_completed: int
    56→    best_metric: float | None
    57→    experiments_remaining: int
    58→
    59→
    60→class ResearchResultResponse(BaseModel):
    61→    """Research result details."""
    62→
    63→    session_id: str
    64→    experiments_completed: int
    65→    best_metric: float | None
    66→    checkpoint_path: str | None
    67→    agent_breakdown: dict[str, Any] | None
    68→    collaboration_insights: list[str]
    69→
    70→
    71→# Active sessions storage (in production: use Redis/SurrealDB)
    72→_active_sessions: dict[str, ResearchAgent] = {}
    73→_active_swarm_sessions: dict[str, ResearchSwarm] = {}
    74→
    75→
    76→@router.post("/start", response_model=ResearchResponse)
    77→async def start_research(
    78→    config: ResearchConfigRequest,
    79→    background_tasks: BackgroundTasks,
    80→):
    81→    """Start autonomous research session.
    82→
    83→    Launches research agent to optimize training configuration.
    84→    """
    85→    try:
    86→        research_config = ResearchConfig(
    87→            experiment_time_budget=config.experiment_time_budget,
    88→            max_experiments=config.max_experiments,
    89→            target_metric=config.target_metric,
    90→            model_depth=config.model_depth,
    91→            vocab_size=config.vocab_size,
    92→            enable_guardrails=config.enable_guardrails,
    93→        )
    94→
    95→        agent = ResearchAgent(config=research_config)
    96→        session_id = agent.session.session_id
    97→
    98→        # Store session
    99→        _active_sessions[session_id] = agent
   100→
   101→        # Start in background
   102→        background_tasks.add_task(agent.run_session)
   103→
   104→        logger.info(f"Started research session: {session_id}")
   105→
   106→        return ResearchResponse(
   107→            session_id=session_id,
   108→            status="running",
   109→            experiments_completed=0,
   110→            best_metric=None,
   111→            experiments_remaining=config.max_experiments,
   112→        )
   113→
   114→    except Exception as e:
   115→        logger.error(f"Failed to start research: {e}")
   116→        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
   117→
   118→
   119→@router.post("/start-multi-agent", response_model=ResearchResponse)
   120→async def start_multi_agent_research(
   121→    config: MultiAgentConfigRequest,
   122→    background_tasks: BackgroundTasks,
   123→):
   124→    """Start multi-agent research session.
   125→
   126→    Coordinates multiple agents with different strategies.
   127→    """
   128→    try:
   129→        multi_config = MultiAgentResearchConfig(
   130→            num_agents=config.num_agents,
   131→            experiments_per_agent=config.experiments_per_agent,
   132→            agent_diversity=config.agent_diversity,
   133→        )
   134→
   135→        swarm = ResearchSwarm(config=multi_config)
   136→        session_id = f"swarm-{datetime.now().isoformat()}"
   137→
   138→        # Store session
   139→        _active_swarm_sessions[session_id] = swarm
   140→
   141→        # Start in background
   142→        import asyncio
   143→
   144→        background_tasks.add_task(lambda: asyncio.create_task(swarm.run_multi_agent_research()))
   145→
   146→        logger.info(f"Started multi-agent research: {session_id}")
   147→
   148→        return ResearchResponse(
   149→            session_id=session_id,
   150→            status="running",
   151→            experiments_completed=0,
   152→            best_metric=None,
   153→            experiments_remaining=config.num_agents * config.experiments_per_agent,
   154→        )
   155→
   156→    except Exception as e:
   157→        logger.error(f"Failed to start multi-agent research: {e}")
   158→        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
   159→
   160→
   161→@router.get("/status/{session_id}", response_model=ResearchResponse)
   162→async def get_research_status(session_id: str):
   163→    """Get research session status."""
   164→    # Check single agent session
   165→    if session_id in _active_sessions:
   166→        agent = _active_sessions[session_id]
   167→
   168→        return ResearchResponse(
   169→            session_id=session_id,
   170→            status="running" if agent.session.active else "completed",
   171→            experiments_completed=agent.session.experiments_completed,
   172→            best_metric=agent.session.best_metric,
   173→            experiments_remaining=agent.config.max_experiments
   174→            - agent.session.experiments_completed,
   175→        )
   176→
   177→    # Check multi-agent session
   178→    if session_id in _active_swarm_sessions:
   179→        swarm = _active_swarm_sessions[session_id]
   180→
   181→        return ResearchResponse(
   182→            session_id=session_id,
   183→            status="running",  # Simplified
   184→            experiments_completed=swarm.results.experiments_completed,
   185→            best_metric=swarm.results.best_metric,
   186→            experiments_remaining=0,  # Would calculate
   187→        )
   188→
   189→    raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Session not found")
   190→
   191→
   192→@router.get("/results/{session_id}", response_model=ResearchResultResponse)
   193→async def get_research_results(session_id: str):
   194→    """Get research session results."""
   195→    # Check single agent session
   196→    if session_id in _active_sessions:
   197→        agent = _active_sessions[session_id]
   198→        best = agent.get_best_result()
   199→
   200→        return ResearchResultResponse(
   201→            session_id=session_id,
   202→            experiments_completed=agent.session.experiments_completed,
   203→            best_metric=best["metric"] if best else None,
   204→            checkpoint_path=best.get("checkpoint") if best else None,
   205→            agent_breakdown=None,
   206→            collaboration_insights=[],
   207→        )
   208→
   209→    # Check multi-agent session
   210→    if session_id in _active_swarm_sessions:
   211→        swarm = _active_swarm_sessions[session_id]
   212→        report = swarm.get_collaboration_report()
   213→
   214→        return ResearchResultResponse(
   215→            session_id=session_id,
   216→            experiments_completed=report["total_experiments"],
   217→            best_metric=report["best_metric"],
   218→            checkpoint_path=None,
   219→            agent_breakdown=report["agent_breakdown"],
   220→            collaboration_insights=report["insights"],
   221→        )
   222→
   223→    raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Session not found")
   224→
   225→
   226→@router.post("/stop/{session_id}")
   227→async def stop_research(session_id: str):
   228→    """Stop research session gracefully."""
   229→    if session_id in _active_sessions:
   230→        agent = _active_sessions[session_id]
   231→        agent.stop()
   232→
   233→        logger.info(f"Stopped research session: {session_id}")
   234→        return {"status": "stopped", "session_id": session_id}
   235→
   236→    if session_id in _active_swarm_sessions:
   237→        # Swarm doesn't have explicit stop yet
   238→        del _active_swarm_sessions[session_id]
   239→
   240→        logger.info(f"Stopped multi-agent session: {session_id}")
   241→        return {"status": "stopped", "session_id": session_id}
   242→
   243→    raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Session not found")
   244→
   245→
   246→@router.get("/experiments/{session_id}")
   247→async def get_experiment_log(session_id: str, limit: int = 100):
   248→    """Get experiment log for a session."""
   249→    if session_id not in _active_sessions:
   250→        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Session not found")
   251→
   252→    agent = _active_sessions[session_id]
   253→
   254→    try:
   255→        # Read experiment log
   256→        log_path = agent.config.experiment_log
   257→
   258→        if not log_path.exists():
   259→            return {"experiments": []}
   260→
   261→        experiments = []
   262→        with open(log_path) as f:
   263→            for line in f:
   264→                if len(experiments) >= limit:
   265→                    break
   266→                try:
   267→                    exp = json.loads(line)
   268→                    experiments.append(exp)
   269→                except json.JSONDecodeError:
   270→                    continue
   271→
   272→        return {"experiments": experiments}
   273→
   274→    except Exception as e:
   275→        logger.error(f"Failed to read experiment log: {e}")
   276→        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
   277→
   278→
   279→@router.get("/dashboard")
   280→async def get_research_dashboard():
   281→    """Get research dashboard overview."""
   282→    active_count = len(_active_sessions) + len(_active_swarm_sessions)
   283→
   284→    return {
   285→        "active_sessions": active_count,
   286→        "single_agent_sessions": len(_active_sessions),
   287→        "multi_agent_sessions": len(_active_swarm_sessions),
   288→        "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
   289→    }
   290→

     1→#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2→"""
     3→COHEZION OVERNIGHT AUTONOMOUS RESEARCH SPRINT (Verified TDD Edition)
     4→==============================================
     5→Grounded in EnhancedSimulator (FLUME + R-Zero).
     6→"""
     7→
     8→import asyncio
     9→import logging
    10→import sys
    11→from datetime import datetime, timedelta
    12→from pathlib import Path
    13→
    14→# Add src to path
    15→PROJECT_ROOT = Path("/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion")
    16→sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT_ROOT / "src"))
    17→
    18→from cohezion.simulation.enhanced_simulator import EnhancedSimulator
    19→from cohezion.swarm.compound_client import get_compound_client
    20→import trackio
    21→
    22→logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s")
    23→logger = logging.getLogger("OvernightMission")
    24→
    25→class OvernightMission:
    26→    def __init__(self):
    27→        self.simulator = EnhancedSimulator()
    28→        self.client = get_compound_client()
    29→        self.end_time = datetime.now() + timedelta(hours=8)
    30→
    31→    async def run_cycle(self, iteration):
    32→        logger.info(f"🌌 Starting Cycle {iteration}...")
    33→        
    34→        # 1. Physics Simulation (Using R-Zero Enhanced Triad)
    35→        # We run a batch of 10 simulations across different streams
    36→        results = await self.simulator.run_batch(10)
    37→        
    38→        stats = self.simulator.get_stats()
    39→        stability = stats.get("avg_score", 0.0)
    40→        logger.info(f"Batch Complete. Avg Stability: {stability:.4f}")
    41→        
    42→        # 2. Slm Research (Analyze stability patterns)
    43→        prompt = f"""
    44→        Analyze these 12D simulation results from our R-Zero Triad:
    45→        Avg Stability: {stability:.4f}
    46→        Approved Ratio: {stats.get('approval_rate', 0):.2f}
    47→        
    48→        Instruction:
    49→        - Identify one 'Manifold Convergence' pattern.
    50→        - Propose a 'Compound Engineering' learning.
    51→        - Format as a Learning for KEY_LEARNINGS.md.
    52→        """
    53→        response, tokens = await self.client.generate(prompt)
    54→        
    55→        # 3. Log to Trackio
    56→        trackio.log({
    57→            "iteration": iteration, 
    58→            "stability": stability,
    59→            "difficulty": stats.get("current_difficulty", 1.0)
    60→        })
    61→        
    62→        # 4. Append Learning (Manifestation)
    63→        with open(PROJECT_ROOT / "src/cohezion/knowledge_graph/KEY_LEARNINGS.md", "a") as f:
    64→            f.write(f"\n### Overnight Learning (Iteration {iteration})\n{response}\n")
    65→            
    66→        logger.info(f"✅ Cycle {iteration} Complete.")
    67→
    68→    async def main_loop(self):
    69→        trackio.init(project="cohezion-core")
    70→        iteration = 1
    71→        while datetime.now() < self.end_time:
    72→            try:
    73→                await self.run_cycle(iteration)
    74→                iteration += 1
    75→            except Exception as e:
    76→                logger.error(f"Cycle failed: {e}", exc_info=True)
    77→            
    78→            # Breathe between cycles
    79→            await asyncio.sleep(600) # 10 min break
    80→            
    81→        trackio.finish()
    82→
    83→if __name__ == "__main__":
    84→    mission = OvernightMission()
    85→    asyncio.run(mission.main_loop())
    86→

     1→"""Research agent configuration.
     2→
     3→Minimal configuration for autonomous training optimization.
     4→"""
     5→
     6→from __future__ import annotations
     7→
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→from typing import Callable
    11→
    12→
    13→@dataclass
    14→class ResearchConfig:
    15→    """Configuration for research agent.
    16→
    17→    Clean, focused config following elegant simplification.
    18→    """
    19→
    20→    # Experiment parameters
    21→    experiment_time_budget: float = 300.0  # 5 minutes in seconds
    22→    max_experiments: int = 100  # Per session
    23→
    24→    # Training parameters
    25→    model_depth: int = 8
    26→    vocab_size: int = 8192
    27→    max_seq_len: int = 1024
    28→    device_batch_size: int = 16
    29→    total_batch_size: int = 524288  # 2**19
    30→
    31→    # Optimization targets
    32→    target_metric: str = "val_bpb"  # validation bits per byte
    33→    metric_direction: str = "minimize"  # lower is better
    34→
    35→    # Agent behavior
    36→    temperature: float = 0.7  # For code generation
    37→    max_code_changes: int = 50  # Lines per experiment
    38→
    39→    # Paths
    40→    train_file: Path = Path("train.py")
    41→    experiment_log: Path = Path("data/research/experiments.jsonl")
    42→    checkpoint_dir: Path = Path("data/research/checkpoints")
    43→
    44→    # Safety
    45→    enable_guardrails: bool = True
    46→    require_human_review: bool = False  # Set True for production
    47→
    48→    def __post_init__(self):
    49→        """Ensure directories exist."""
    50→        self.experiment_log.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    51→        self.checkpoint_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    52→
    53→
    54→@dataclass
    55→class ExperimentResult:
    56→    """Result of a single experiment.
    57→
    58→    Minimal data class for tracking experiment outcomes.
    59→    """
    60→
    61→    experiment_id: str
    62→    timestamp: str
    63→    metric_value: float
    64→    metric_name: str
    65→    improved: bool
    66→    code_changes: list[str]
    67→    duration_seconds: float
    68→    checkpoint_path: Path | None = None
    69→
    70→    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
    71→        """Convert to dictionary for logging."""
    72→        return {
    73→            "experiment_id": self.experiment_id,
    74→            "timestamp": self.timestamp,
    75→            "metric_value": self.metric_value,
    76→            "metric_name": self.metric_name,
    77→            "improved": self.improved,
    78→            "code_changes": self.code_changes,
    79→            "duration_seconds": self.duration_seconds,
    80→            "checkpoint_path": str(self.checkpoint_path) if self.checkpoint_path else None,
    81→        }
    82→

   198→                ngrok_api_key=ngrok_api_key,
   199→                fallback_ollama_url=ollama_base_url,
   200→                enable_failover=enable_ngrok_failover,
   201→            )
   202→            logger.info(f"TokenEfficientClient using ngrok gateway: {ngrok_endpoint}")
   203→        else:
   204→            self.ollama = ResilientOllamaClient(base_url=ollama_base_url)
   205→

   207→        self.config = config or CohezionConfig()
   208→        self.semantic_threshold = semantic_threshold

   212→            persistent_cache = PersistentTokenCache(
   213→                cache_dir=cache_dir, persistence_enabled=True, auto_restore=True
   214→            )
   215→            self.batch_processor = BatchProcessor(self, self.config, cache=persistent_cache)

   218→
   219→        # Initialize L2 semantic cache (non-blocking)

   222→            try:

   226→                    max_entries=1000,
   227→                    cache_dir=cache_dir,

   232→                self.semantic_cache = None

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/src/cohezion/research/agent.py",
  "old_string": "    def _execute_training(self, time_budget: float) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Execute training with time limit.\n\n        Runs autoresearch-style training for fixed duration.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Run training script\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [\n                \"python\",\n                \"train.py\",",
  "new_string": "    def _execute_training(self, time_budget: float) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Execute training with time limit.\n\n        Runs autoresearch-style training for fixed duration.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Resolve train script relative to project root\n        project_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent\n        train_script = (project_root / self.config.train_file).resolve()\n        if not str(train_script).startswith(str(project_root)):\n            raise ValueError(f\"Train script outside project root: {self.config.train_file}\")\n\n        # Run security guardrails if enabled\n        if self.config.enable_guardrails and train_script.exists():\n            from cohezion.research.security import ResearchSecurityGuardrails, CodeChange\n\n            guardrails = ResearchSecurityGuardrails()\n            change = CodeChange(\n                file_path=train_script,\n                old_code=\"\",\n                new_code=train_script.read_text(),\n                change_type=\"modify\",\n            )\n            validation = guardrails.validate_change(change)\n            if not validation.is_valid:\n                raise RuntimeError(f\"Guardrail blocked execution: {validation.issues}\")\n\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [\n                \"python\",\n                str(train_script),"
}

{
