    23→
    24→
    25→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    26→
    27→
    28→@dataclass
    29→class ConsensusVote:
    30→    """Individual vote in democratic consensus."""
    31→
    32→    voter_id: str
    33→    vote: str  # "improve", "maintain", "revert"
    34→    confidence: float  # 0.0 - 1.0
    35→    reasoning: str
    36→    timestamp: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().isoformat())
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class ConsensusResult:
    41→    """Result of democratic consensus."""
    42→
    43→    proposal: str
    44→    votes_for: int
    45→    votes_against: int
    46→    votes_abstain: int
    47→    winning_vote: str
    48→    confidence: float
    49→    consensus_achieved: bool
    50→    timestamp: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().isoformat())
    51→
    52→
    53→class PartyModeConsensus:
    54→    """Democratic consensus system for autonomous improvements.
    55→
    56→    Multiple agents vote on whether to apply improvements.
    57→    No single point of failure.
    58→    """
    59→
    60→    def __init__(self, min_agents: int = 3, consensus_threshold: float = 0.66):
    61→        """Initialize consensus system.
    62→
    63→        Args:
    64→            min_agents: Minimum agents needed for quorum
    65→            consensus_threshold: % of votes needed for consensus
    66→        """
    67→        self.min_agents = min_agents
    68→        self.consensus_threshold = consensus_threshold
    69→        self.voting_history: list[ConsensusResult] = []
    70→
    71→        # Party agents with different perspectives
    72→        self.agents = {
    73→            "optimist": self._optimist_vote,
    74→            "pessimist": self._pessimist_vote,
    75→            "pragmatist": self._pragmatist_vote,
    76→            "thermodynamicist": self._thermodynamicist_vote,
    77→            "economist": self._economist_vote,
    78→        }
    79→
    80→    def _optimist_vote(self, proposal: str, metrics: dict[str, Any]) -> ConsensusVote:
    81→        """Optimist agent - votes for improvement if any benefit."""
    82→        improvement = metrics.get("improvement_pct", 0)
    83→        if improvement > 5:
    84→            return ConsensusVote(
    85→                voter_id="optimist",
    86→                vote="improve",
    87→                confidence=min(improvement / 10, 1.0),
    88→                reasoning=f"{improvement:.1f}% improvement detected",
    89→            )
    90→        return ConsensusVote(
    91→            voter_id="optimist",
    92→            vote="maintain",
    93→            confidence=0.5,
    94→            reasoning="No significant improvement",
    95→        )
    96→
    97→    def _pessimist_vote(self, proposal: str, metrics: dict[str, Any]) -> ConsensusVote:
    98→        """Pessimist agent - cautious, needs strong evidence."""
    99→        improvement = metrics.get("improvement_pct", 0)
   100→        cost = metrics.get("cost_usd", 0)
   101→
   102→        if improvement > 15 and cost < 5:
   103→            return ConsensusVote(
   104→                voter_id="pessimist",
   105→                vote="improve",
   106→                confidence=0.7,
   107→                reasoning="Strong improvement with low cost",
   108→            )
   109→        return ConsensusVote(
   110→            voter_id="pessimist",
   111→            vote="revert",
   112→            confidence=0.8,
   113→            reasoning="Insufficient evidence or too expensive",
   114→        )
   115→
   116→    def _pragmatist_vote(self, proposal: str, metrics: dict[str, Any]) -> ConsensusVote:
   117→        """Pragmatist agent - balances risk and reward."""
   118→        improvement = metrics.get("improvement_pct", 0)
   119→        reproducibility = metrics.get("cv", 1.0)  # Coefficient of variation
   120→
   121→        if improvement > 10 and reproducibility < 0.1:
   122→            return ConsensusVote(
   123→                voter_id="pragmatist",
   124→                vote="improve",
   125→                confidence=0.85,
   126→                reasoning="Reproducible 10%+ improvement",
   127→            )
   128→        return ConsensusVote(
   129→            voter_id="pragmatist",
   130→            vote="maintain",
   131→            confidence=0.6,
   132→            reasoning="Not reproducible enough",
   133→        )
   134→
   135→    def _thermodynamicist_vote(self, proposal: str, metrics: dict[str, Any]) -> ConsensusVote:
   136→        """Thermodynamicist agent - cares about system stability."""
   137→        entropy = metrics.get("thermodynamic_entropy", 0)
   138→        coherence = metrics.get("coherence_mean", 0)
   139→
   140→        if entropy > 0 and coherence > 0.6:
   141→            return ConsensusVote(
   142→                voter_id="thermodynamicist",
   143→                vote="improve",
   144→                confidence=min(coherence, 1.0),
   145→                reasoning=f"Entropy positive ({entropy:.3f}), coherence high ({coherence:.2f})",
   146→            )
   147→        return ConsensusVote(
   148→            voter_id="thermodynamicist",
   149→            vote="revert",
   150→            confidence=0.9,
   151→            reasoning="Thermodynamic instability detected",
   152→        )
   153→
   154→    def _economist_vote(self, proposal: str, metrics: dict[str, Any]) -> ConsensusVote:
   155→        """Economist agent - cares about cost efficiency."""
   156→        improvement = metrics.get("improvement_pct", 0)
   157→        cost = metrics.get("cost_usd", 0)
   158→        experiments = metrics.get("experiments_run", 1)
   159→
   160→        efficiency = improvement / max(cost, 0.01)  # Improvement per dollar
   161→
   162→        if efficiency > 5:  # >5% per dollar
   163→            return ConsensusVote(
   164→                voter_id="economist",
   165→                vote="improve",
   166→                confidence=min(efficiency / 10, 1.0),
   167→                reasoning=f"Efficient: {efficiency:.1f}% per ${cost:.2f}",
   168→            )
   169→        return ConsensusVote(
   170→            voter_id="economist",
   171→            vote="maintain",
   172→            confidence=0.7,
   173→            reasoning=f"Low efficiency: {efficiency:.1f}% per ${cost:.2f}",
   174→        )
   175→
   176→    def vote(self, proposal: str, metrics: dict[str, Any]) -> ConsensusResult:
   177→        """Run democratic consensus on proposal.
   178→
   179→        Args:
   180→            proposal: Description of proposed change
   181→            metrics: Dict of metrics to evaluate
   182→
   183→        Returns:
   184→            ConsensusResult with voting outcome
   185→        """
   186→        # Collect votes from all agents
   187→        votes = []
   188→        for agent_name, vote_fn in self.agents.items():
   189→            try:
   190→                vote = vote_fn(proposal, metrics)
   191→                votes.append(vote)
   192→                logger.info(f"Agent {agent_name} voted: {vote.vote} ({vote.confidence:.2f})")
   193→            except Exception as e:
   194→                logger.warning(f"Agent {agent_name} failed to vote: {e}")
   195→
   196→        if len(votes) < self.min_agents:
   197→            return ConsensusResult(
   198→                proposal=proposal,
   199→                votes_for=0,
   200→                votes_against=0,
   201→                votes_abstain=len(votes),
   202→                winning_vote="abstain",
   203→                confidence=0.0,
   204→                consensus_achieved=False,
   205→            )
   206→
   207→        # Count votes
   208→        vote_counts = {"improve": 0, "maintain": 0, "revert": 0}
   209→        total_confidence = 0.0
   210→
   211→        for vote in votes:
   212→            vote_counts[vote.vote] += 1
   213→            total_confidence += vote.confidence
   214→
   215→        # Determine winner
   216→        winning_vote = max(vote_counts, key=vote_counts.get)
   217→        max_votes = vote_counts[winning_vote]
   218→
   219→        # Check consensus threshold
   220→        consensus_achieved = max_votes / len(votes) >= self.consensus_threshold
   221→
   222→        avg_confidence = total_confidence / len(votes)
   223→
   224→        result = ConsensusResult(
   225→            proposal=proposal,
   226→            votes_for=vote_counts["improve"],
   227→            votes_against=vote_counts["revert"],
   228→            votes_abstain=vote_counts["maintain"],
   229→            winning_vote=winning_vote,
   230→            confidence=avg_confidence,
   231→            consensus_achieved=consensus_achieved,
   232→        )
   233→
   234→        self.voting_history.append(result)
   235→
   236→        logger.info(
   237→            f"Consensus result: {winning_vote} "
   238→            f"({max_votes}/{len(votes)} votes, "
   239→            f"consensus={'achieved' if consensus_achieved else 'failed'})"
   240→        )
   241→
   242→        return result
   243→
   244→
   245→class Orborous:
   246→    """Self-improving compound system that never ends.
   247→
   248→    The recursive loop:
   249→    1. Monitor compound system
   250→    2. Detect degradation
   251→    3. Research Squad runs experiments
   252→    4. Party Mode consensus votes
   253→    5. Apply improvements
   254→    6. Validate
   255→    7. Repeat
   256→
   257→    The ouroboros eating its own tail - infinite self-improvement.
   258→    """
   259→
   260→    def __init__(
   261→        self,
   262→        squad: ResearchSquad | None = None,
   263→        consensus: PartyModeConsensus | None = None,
   264→        cost_budget: CostBudget | None = None,
   265→    ):
   266→        """Initialize Orborous self-improvement system."""
   267→        self.squad = squad or integrate_with_compound_system()
   268→        self.consensus = consensus or PartyModeConsensus()
   269→        self.cost_budget = cost_budget or CostBudget(max_cost_usd=50.0)
   270→
   271→        self.improvement_history: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   272→        self.cycle_count = 0
   273→        self.active = False
   274→
   275→        logger.info("🐍 Orborous initialized - self-improvement system ready")
   276→
   277→    async def monitor_cycle(self) -> None:
   278→        """Single self-improvement cycle."""
   279→        self.cycle_count += 1
   280→        logger.info(f"🔄 Orborous Cycle #{self.cycle_count} starting")
   281→
   282→        try:
   283→            # 1. Monitor compound system
   284→            logger.info("Monitoring compound system...")
   285→            skill_metrics = await self._get_compound_metrics()
   286→
   287→            # 2. Detect degradation
   288→            degraded_skills = []
   289→            for skill_name, metrics in skill_metrics.items():
   290→                signal = self.squad.detect_degradation(skill_name, metrics)
   291→                if signal:
   292→                    degraded_skills.append(signal)
   293→                    logger.warning(f"🔍 Degradation detected: {skill_name}")
   294→
   295→            if not degraded_skills:
   296→                logger.info("✅ No degradation detected, system healthy")
   297→                return
   298→
   299→            # 3. Research Squad optimizes
   300→            for signal in degraded_skills[:3]:  # Max 3 per cycle
   301→                logger.info(f"🔬 Optimizing: {signal.skill_name}")
   302→
   303→                result = self.squad.optimize_skill(
   304→                    skill_name=signal.skill_name,
   305→                    baseline_metric=signal.current_value,
   306→                    max_experiments=20,
   307→                )
   308→
   309→                # 4. Party Mode consensus
   310→                logger.info("🗳️  Calling democratic consensus...")
   311→                consensus_result = self.consensus.vote(
   312→                    proposal=f"Apply refinement to {result.target_skill}",
   313→                    metrics=result.to_dict(),
   314→                )
   315→
   316→                # 5. Apply if consensus achieved
   317→                if consensus_result.consensus_achieved:
   318→                    if consensus_result.winning_vote == "improve":
   319→                        logger.info("✅ Consensus achieved - applying refinement")
   320→                        success = self.squad.apply_refinement(result)
   321→                        if success:
   322→                            self.improvement_history.append(
   323→                                {
   324→                                    "cycle": self.cycle_count,
   325→                                    "skill": result.target_skill,
   326→                                    "improvement_pct": result.improvement_pct,
   327→                                    "consensus_confidence": consensus_result.confidence,
   328→                                }
   329→                            )
   330→                    elif consensus_result.winning_vote == "revert":
   331→                        logger.info("❌ Consensus rejected - rolling back")
   332→                        # Rollback logic here
   333→                else:
   334→                    logger.info("⚠️  No consensus - maintaining current state")
   335→
   336→            # 6. Validate
   337→            logger.info("Validating improvements...")
   338→            await self._validate_improvements()
   339→
   340→        except Exception as e:
   341→            logger.error(f"Orborous cycle failed: {e}")
   342→            # Don't stop - keep improving
   343→
   344→    async def _get_compound_metrics(self) -> dict[str, dict[str, float]]:
   345→        """Get current metrics from compound system."""
   346→        # In production: Query GlobalMetricsAggregator
   347→        # For now: Simulate realistic metrics
   348→        return {
   349→            "coding": {
   350→                "coherence": 0.45,  # Degraded
   351→                "success_rate": 0.95,
   352→            },
   353→            "analysis": {
   354→                "coherence": 0.82,
   355→                "success_rate": 0.88,
   356→            },
   357→            "security": {
   358→                "coherence": 0.91,
   359→                "success_rate": 0.97,
   360→            },
   361→        }
   362→
   363→    async def _validate_improvements(self) -> None:
   364→        """Validate improvements were actually improvements."""
   365→        # Run thermodynamic checks
   366→        # Verify no regressions
   367→        logger.info("✓ Validation complete")
   368→
   369→    async def run_forever(self) -> None:
   370→        """Run Orborous - infinite self-improvement."""
   371→        logger.info("🐍 Orborous awakening - infinite self-improvement engaged")
   372→        self.active = True
   373→
   374→        while self.active:
   375→            await self.monitor_cycle()
   376→
   377→            # Sleep between cycles
   378→            await asyncio.sleep(300)  # 5 minutes
   379→
   380→            # Safety: Check cost
   381→            if self.squad.cost_tracker.total_cost > self.cost_budget.max_cost_usd:
   382→                logger.warning("💰 Budget exceeded - pausing Orborous")
   383→                self.active = False
   384→
   385→    def stop(self) -> None:
   386→        """Gracefully stop Orborous."""
   387→        logger.info("🛑 Orborous stopping...")
   388→        self.active = False
   389→
   390→    def get_status(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   391→        """Get Orborous status report."""
   392→        return {
   393→            "cycles_completed": self.cycle_count,
   394→            "active": self.active,
   395→            "improvements_made": len(self.improvement_history),
   396→            "total_cost_usd": self.squad.cost_tracker.total_cost,
   397→            "budget_remaining_pct": (
   398→                1 - self.squad.cost_tracker.total_cost / self.cost_budget.max_cost_usd
   399→            )
   400→            * 100,
   401→            "latest_improvements": self.improvement_history[-5:],
   402→        }
   403→
   404→
   405→# Orborous activation
   406→async def awaken_orborous():
   407→    """Awaken the self-improving system."""
   408→    orborous = Orborous()
   409→    await orborous.run_forever()
   410→
   411→
   412→# Entry point
   413→if __name__ == "__main__":
   414→    # Run Orborous
   415→    asyncio.run(awaken_orborous())
   416→

    55→@dataclass
    56→class DegradationSignal:
    57→    """Signal indicating potential optimization opportunity."""
    58→

    60→    metric_name: str
    61→    current_value: float
    62→    threshold: float

    70→
    71→class ResearchSquad:
    72→    """Autonomous squad for compound system optimization.
    73→
    74→    The Research Squad monitors the compound system for degradation,
    75→    runs experiments to find optimizations, and applies refinements
    76→    through the skill refinement pipeline.
    77→
    78→    Elegant integration: ~200 lines, fully self-contained.
    79→    """
    80→
    81→    def __init__(
    82→        self,
    83→        swarm: Swarm | None = None,
    84→        executor: CompoundExecutor | None = None,
    85→        cost_budget: CostBudget | None = None,
    86→    ):
    87→        """Initialize Research Squad.
    88→
    89→        Args:
    90→            swarm: Swarm for multi-agent coordination
    91→            executor: CompoundExecutor for running experiments
    92→            cost_budget: Budget for optimization sessions
    93→        """
    94→        self.swarm = swarm or Swarm()
    95→        self.executor = executor or CompoundExecutor(
    96→            execute_fn=self._run_optimization_experiment,
    97→            config=ExecutionConfig(max_retries=1),
    98→        )
    99→        self.cost_tracker = CostTracker(budget=cost_budget or CostBudget(max_cost_usd=10.0))
   100→        self.degradation_thresholds = {
   101→            "coherence": 0.5,
   102→            "success_rate": 0.75,
   103→            "token_efficiency": 0.5,
   104→        }
   105→        self.optimization_history: list[OptimizationResult] = []
   106→
   107→        logger.info("Research Squad initialized")
   108→
   109→    def _run_optimization_experiment(
   110→        self, task: Task, context: dict[str, Any]
   111→    ) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   112→        """Execute a single optimization experiment.
   113→
   114→        This is the core execution function passed to CompoundExecutor.
   115→        """
   116→        skill_name = task.metadata.get("skill_name", "unknown")
   117→        experiment_config = task.metadata.get("config", {})
   118→
   119→        # Simulate experiment: test different skill configurations
   120→        # In production, would actually execute skill with varied params
   121→        import random
   122→
   123→        baseline = experiment_config.get("baseline_metric", 1.0)
   124→
   125→        # Simulate improvement
   126→        improvement = random.uniform(0.0, 0.2)
   127→        new_metric = baseline * (1 - improvement)
   128→
   129→        return (
   130→            f"Experiment for {skill_name}: metric={new_metric:.3f}",
   131→            {
   132→                "metric_value": new_metric,
   133→                "improvement": improvement,
   134→                "skill_name": skill_name,
   135→            },
   136→        )
   137→
   138→    def detect_degradation(
   139→        self, skill_name: str, metrics: dict[str, float]
   140→    ) -> DegradationSignal | None:
   141→        """Detect if a skill needs optimization.
   142→
   143→        Args:
   144→            skill_name: Name of skill to check
   145→            metrics: Current metrics dict
   146→
   147→        Returns:
   148→            DegradationSignal if optimization needed, None otherwise
   149→        """
   150→        signals = []
   151→
   152→        # Check coherence
   153→        coherence = metrics.get("coherence", 1.0)
   154→        if coherence < self.degradation_thresholds["coherence"]:
   155→            severity = "critical" if coherence < 0.4 else "high"
   156→            signals.append(
   157→                DegradationSignal(
   158→                    skill_name=skill_name,
   159→                    metric_name="coherence",
   160→                    current_value=coherence,
   161→                    threshold=self.degradation_thresholds["coherence"],
   162→                    severity=severity,
   163→                )
   164→            )
   165→
   166→        # Check success rate
   167→        success_rate = metrics.get("success_rate", 1.0)
   168→        if success_rate < self.degradation_thresholds["success_rate"]:
   169→            severity = "critical" if success_rate < 0.6 else "high"
   170→            signals.append(
   171→                DegradationSignal(
   172→                    skill_name=skill_name,
   173→                    metric_name="success_rate",
   174→                    current_value=success_rate,
   175→                    threshold=self.degradation_thresholds["success_rate"],
   176→                    severity=severity,
   177→                )
   178→            )
   179→
   180→        # Return most severe signal
   181→        if signals:
   182→            critical = [s for s in signals if s.severity == "critical"]
   183→            return critical[0] if critical else signals[0]
   184→
   185→        return None
   186→
   187→    def optimize_skill(
   188→        self,
   189→        skill_name: str,
   190→        baseline_metric: float,
   191→        max_experiments: int = 20,
   192→    ) -> OptimizationResult:
   193→        """Run optimization experiments on a skill.
   194→
   195→        Args:
   196→            skill_name: Skill to optimize
   197→            baseline_metric: Current performance metric
   198→            max_experiments: Maximum experiments to run
   199→
   200→        Returns:
   201→            OptimizationResult with findings
   202→        """
   203→        import time
   204→
   205→        start_time = time.time()
   206→
   207→        logger.info(f"Starting optimization for {skill_name}: baseline={baseline_metric:.3f}")
   208→
   209→        # Create research config
   210→        config = ResearchConfig(
   211→            experiment_time_budget=10.0,
   212→            max_experiments=max_experiments,
   213→            target_metric="coherence",
   214→        )
   215→
   216→        # Create agent for this optimization
   217→        agent = ResearchAgent(config=config, executor=self.executor)
   218→
   219→        # Run research session
   220→        session = agent.run_session()
   221→
   222→        # Calculate results (simulated - in production would analyze actual experiments)
   223→        import random
   224→
   225→        best_improvement = random.uniform(0.05, 0.25)
   226→        best_metric = baseline_metric * (1 - best_improvement)
   227→
   228→        elapsed = time.time() - start_time
   229→
   230→        # Generate learnings
   231→        learnings = [
   232→            f"Found {best_improvement * 100:.1f}% improvement in {session.experiments_completed} experiments",
   233→            f"Optimal configuration discovered through systematic search",
   234→            f"Cost: ${self.cost_tracker.total_cost:.2f}",
   235→        ]
   236→
   237→        result = OptimizationResult(
   238→            target_skill=skill_name,
   239→            optimized=best_improvement > 0.1,  # >10% improvement
   240→            before_metric=baseline_metric,
   241→            after_metric=best_metric,
   242→            improvement_pct=best_improvement * 100,
   243→            experiments_run=session.experiments_completed,
   244→            cost_usd=self.cost_tracker.total_cost,
   245→            wall_time_seconds=elapsed,
   246→            learnings=learnings,
   247→            refinement_applied=False,  # Would be set by SkillRefiner
   248→        )
   249→
   250→        self.optimization_history.append(result)
   251→
   252→        logger.info(
   253→            f"Optimization complete: {skill_name} improved by {result.improvement_pct:.1f}%"
   254→        )
   255→
   256→        return result
   257→
   258→    def apply_refinement(self, result: OptimizationResult) -> bool:
   259→        """Apply optimization result via skill refinement pipeline.
   260→
   261→        Args:
   262→            result: OptimizationResult to apply
   263→
   264→        Returns:
   265→            True if refinement applied successfully
   266→        """
   267→        try:
   268→            # In production, would call SkillRefiner
   269→            # For now, simulate successful refinement
   270→            logger.info(f"Applying refinement for {result.target_skill}")
   271→            result.refinement_applied = True
   272→            return True
   273→        except Exception as e:
   274→            logger.error(f"Failed to apply refinement: {e}")
   275→            return False
   276→
   277→    def run_optimization_cycle(
   278→        self, skill_metrics: dict[str, dict[str, float]]
   279→    ) -> list[OptimizationResult]:
   280→        """Run full optimization cycle on degraded skills.
   281→
   282→        Args:
   283→            skill_metrics: Dict mapping skill names to their metrics
   284→
   285→        Returns:
   286→            List of OptimizationResults
   287→        """
   288→        results = []
   289→
   290→        for skill_name, metrics in skill_metrics.items():
   291→            # Check for degradation
   292→            signal = self.detect_degradation(skill_name, metrics)
   293→
   294→            if signal:
   295→                logger.warning(
   296→                    f"Degradation detected: {skill_name} - {signal.metric_name}="
   297→                    f"{signal.current_value:.3f} (threshold={signal.threshold:.3f})"
   298→                )
   299→
   300→                # Run optimization
   301→                result = self.optimize_skill(
   302→                    skill_name=skill_name,
   303→                    baseline_metric=signal.current_value,
   304→                )
   305→
   306→                # Apply if significant improvement
   307→                if result.improvement_pct >= 10.0:  # 10% threshold
   308→                    if self.apply_refinement(result):
   309→                        logger.info(f"Refinement applied for {skill_name}")
   310→
   311→                results.append(result)
   312→
   313→        return results
   314→
   315→    def get_optimization_report(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   316→        """Generate comprehensive optimization report."""
   317→        if not self.optimization_history:
   318→            return {"status": "no_optimizations_run"}
   319→
   320→        total_cost = sum(r.cost_usd for r in self.optimization_history)
   321→        total_improvement = sum(r.improvement_pct for r in self.optimization_history)
   322→        avg_improvement = total_improvement / len(self.optimization_history)
   323→
   324→        return {
   325→            "total_optimizations": len(self.optimization_history),
   326→            "total_cost_usd": round(total_cost, 2),
   327→            "total_improvement_pct": round(total_improvement, 2),
   328→            "average_improvement_pct": round(avg_improvement, 2),
   329→            "successful_refinements": sum(
   330→                1 for r in self.optimization_history if r.refinement_applied
   331→            ),
   332→            "optimizations": [r.to_dict() for r in self.optimization_history],
   333→        }
   334→
   335→    def auto_optimize(self, metrics_source: Callable[[], dict[str, dict[str, float]]]) -> None:
   336→        """Continuous auto-optimization loop.
   337→
   338→        Args:
   339→            metrics_source: Callable that returns current skill metrics
   340→        """
   341→        import time
   342→
   343→        logger.info("Starting auto-optimization loop")
   344→
   345→        while True:
   346→            try:
   347→                # Get current metrics
   348→                skill_metrics = metrics_source()
   349→
   350→                # Run optimization cycle
   351→                results = self.run_optimization_cycle(skill_metrics)
   352→
   353→                if results:
   354→                    logger.info(f"Optimization cycle complete: {len(results)} skills optimized")
   355→
   356→                # Sleep before next check (every 5 minutes)
   357→                time.sleep(300)
   358→
   359→            except Exception as e:
   360→                logger.error(f"Auto-optimization error: {e}")
   361→                time.sleep(60)  # Shorter sleep on error
   362→
   363→
   364→# Integration function
   365→def integrate_with_compound_system() -> ResearchSquad:
   366→    """Factory function to integrate Research Squad with compound system.
   367→
   368→    Returns:
   369→        Configured ResearchSquad instance
   370→    """
   371→    squad = ResearchSquad(
   372→        cost_budget=CostBudget(
   373→            max_cost_usd=10.0,
   374→            max_experiments=50,
   375→            hard_limit=True,
   376→        ),
   377→    )
   378→
   379→    logger.info("Research Squad integrated with compound system")
   380→    return squad
   381→
   382→
   383→# Example usage
   384→if __name__ == "__main__":
   385→    # Create squad
   386→    squad = integrate_with_compound_system()
   387→
   388→    # Example: Optimize a degraded skill

   401→
   402→    if results:
   403→        print(f"Optimization complete: {len(results)} skills improved")
   404→        report = squad.get_optimization_report()
   405→        print(f"Total cost: ${report['total_cost_usd']:.2f}")
   406→        print(f"Average improvement: {report['average_improvement_pct']:.1f}%")
   407→    else:
   408→        print("No optimizations needed")
   409→

     1→"""Omnibus - The Master Gateway Controller.
     2→
     3→Spawns specialized squads to optimize every component of the Cohezion ecosystem.
     4→"""
     5→
     6→from __future__ import annotations
     7→
     8→import asyncio
     9→import json
    10→import logging
    11→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    12→from datetime import datetime
    13→from pathlib import Path
    14→from typing import Any
    15→
    16→from cohezion.research.orborous import Orborous, PartyModeConsensus
    17→
    18→
    19→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    20→
    21→
    22→@dataclass
    23→class GatewayStatus:
    24→    """Status of a single gateway."""
    25→
    26→    name: str
    27→    status: str  # "locked", "unlocking", "unlocked"
    28→    health_score: float  # 0.0 - 1.0
    29→    last_optimized: str
    30→    improvements_made: int
    31→    cost_usd: float
    32→    active: bool = True
    33→
    34→    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
    35→        return {
    36→            "name": self.name,
    37→            "status": self.status,
    38→            "health_score": round(self.health_score, 3),
    39→            "last_optimized": self.last_optimized,
    40→            "improvements_made": self.improvements_made,
    41→            "cost_usd": round(self.cost_usd, 2),
    42→            "active": self.active,
    43→        }
    44→
    45→
    46→class Omnibus:
    47→    """The Master Gateway Controller.
    48→
    49→    Omnibus unlocks and optimizes all 9 major gateways:
    50→    1. Research (✓ Already unlocked via Orborous)
    51→    2. Cache - Semantic and token caching optimization
    52→    3. Security - Pipeline and guardrail improvements
    53→    4. Vault - MCP vault persistence optimization
    54→    5. Swarm - Multi-agent coordination improvements
    55→    6. Universe - 12D manifold optimization
    56→    7. FLUME - VAE hyperparameter optimization
    57→    8. Skills - PRIME skill refinement
    58→    9. API - Endpoint and routing optimization
    59→
    60→    The 10th gateway is Omnibus itself - self-optimization of the optimizer.
    61→    """
    62→
    63→    def __init__(self):
    64→        """Initialize Omnibus master controller."""
    65→        self.gateways: dict[str, GatewayStatus] = {}
    66→        self.squads: dict[str, Orborous] = {}
    67→        self.active = False
    68→        self.cycle_count = 0
    69→
    70→        # Initialize all gateways
    71→        self._init_gateways()
    72→
    73→        logger.info("🌟 Omnibus initialized - Master Gateway Controller ready")
    74→
    75→    def _init_gateways(self) -> None:
    76→        """Initialize all 9 gateways."""
    77→        gateway_names = [
    78→            "research",
    79→            "cache",
    80→            "security",
    81→            "vault",
    82→            "swarm",
    83→            "universe",
    84→            "flume",
    85→            "skills",
    86→            "api",
    87→        ]
    88→
    89→        for name in gateway_names:
    90→            self.gateways[name] = GatewayStatus(
    91→                name=name,
    92→                status="locked" if name != "research" else "unlocked",
    93→                health_score=0.5 if name != "research" else 0.95,
    94→                last_optimized=datetime.now().isoformat() if name == "research" else "never",
    95→                improvements_made=0 if name != "research" else 1,
    96→                cost_usd=0.0,
    97→            )
    98→
    99→        logger.info(f"Initialized {len(gateway_names)} gateways")
   100→
   101→    async def unlock_gateway(self, gateway_name: str) -> bool:
   102→        """Unlock a specific gateway.
   103→
   104→        Args:
   105→            gateway_name: Name of gateway to unlock
   106→
   107→        Returns:
   108→            True if successfully unlocked
   109→        """
   110→        if gateway_name not in self.gateways:
   111→            logger.error(f"Unknown gateway: {gateway_name}")
   112→            return False
   113→
   114→        gateway = self.gateways[gateway_name]
   115→
   116→        if gateway.status == "unlocked":
   117→            logger.info(f"Gateway '{gateway_name}' already unlocked")
   118→            return True
   119→
   120→        logger.info(f"🔓 Unlocking gateway: {gateway_name}")
   121→        gateway.status = "unlocking"
   122→
   123→        try:
   124→            # Spawn specialized squad for this gateway
   125→            squad = await self._spawn_squad(gateway_name)
   126→            self.squads[gateway_name] = squad
   127→
   128→            # Run initial optimization
   129→            await squad.monitor_cycle()
   130→
   131→            gateway.status = "unlocked"
   132→            gateway.health_score = 0.85
   133→            gateway.last_optimized = datetime.now().isoformat()
   134→            gateway.improvements_made += 1
   135→
   136→            logger.info(f"✅ Gateway '{gateway_name}' unlocked successfully")
   137→            return True
   138→
   139→        except Exception as e:
   140→            logger.error(f"Failed to unlock gateway '{gateway_name}': {e}")
   141→            gateway.status = "locked"
   142→            return False
   143→
   144→    async def _spawn_squad(self, gateway_name: str) -> Orborous:
   145→        """Spawn a specialized squad for a gateway.
   146→
   147→        Args:
   148→            gateway_name: Name of gateway
   149→
   150→        Returns:
   151→            Configured Orborous instance
   152→        """
   153→        from cohezion.research import integrate_with_compound_system
   154→        from cohezion.research.cost_optimization import CostBudget
   155→
   156→        # Create specialized squad based on gateway type
   157→        squad = Orborous(
   158→            cost_budget=CostBudget(max_cost_usd=10.0),
   159→        )
   160→
   161→        logger.info(f"Spawned squad for gateway: {gateway_name}")
   162→        return squad
   163→
   164→    async def run_master_cycle(self) -> None:
   165→        """Run one cycle of omnibus - check all gateways."""
   166→        self.cycle_count += 1
   167→        logger.info(f"🌟 Omnibus Cycle #{self.cycle_count}")
   168→
   169→        # Check each unlocked gateway
   170→        for name, gateway in self.gateways.items():
   171→            if gateway.status == "unlocked" and name in self.squads:
   172→                logger.info(f"Monitoring gateway: {name}")
   173→                try:
   174→                    await self.squads[name].monitor_cycle()
   175→                    gateway.health_score = min(1.0, gateway.health_score + 0.01)
   176→                except Exception as e:
   177→                    logger.error(f"Gateway '{name}' optimization failed: {e}")
   178→                    gateway.health_score = max(0.0, gateway.health_score - 0.1)
   179→
   180→        # Try to unlock next locked gateway
   181→        locked = [n for n, g in self.gateways.items() if g.status == "locked"]
   182→        if locked:
   183→            next_gateway = locked[0]
   184→            logger.info(f"Attempting to unlock next gateway: {next_gateway}")
   185→            await self.unlock_gateway(next_gateway)
   186→
   187→    async def run_forever(self) -> None:
   188→        """Run Omnibus indefinitely."""
   189→        logger.info("🌟 Omnibus awakening - unlocking all gateways")
   190→        self.active = True
   191→
   192→        # First, ensure research gateway is active
   193→        if self.gateways["research"].status != "unlocked":
   194→            await self.unlock_gateway("research")
   195→
   196→        while self.active:
   197→            await self.run_master_cycle()
   198→            await asyncio.sleep(600)  # 10 minutes between cycles
   199→
   200→    def get_master_status(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   201→        """Get status of all gateways."""
   202→        unlocked = sum(1 for g in self.gateways.values() if g.status == "unlocked")
   203→        locked = sum(1 for g in self.gateways.values() if g.status == "locked")
   204→        total_health = sum(g.health_score for g in self.gateways.values())
   205→
   206→        return {
   207→            "omnibus_cycles": self.cycle_count,
   208→            "gateways_unlocked": unlocked,
   209→            "gateways_locked": locked,
   210→            "total_health": round(total_health / len(self.gateways), 3),
   211→            "gateways": {name: g.to_dict() for name, g in self.gateways.items()},
   212→        }
   213→
   214→    def get_gateway_dashboard(self) -> str:
   215→        """Generate visual dashboard of all gateways."""
   216→        lines = [
   217→            "╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗",
   218→            "║              OMNIBUS GATEWAY DASHBOARD                   ║",
   219→            "╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣",
   220→        ]
   221→
   222→        for name, gateway in self.gateways.items():
   223→            status_icon = {
   224→                "unlocked": "✅",
   225→                "unlocking": "🔓",
   226→                "locked": "🔒",
   227→            }.get(gateway.status, "❓")
   228→
   229→            health_bar = self._render_health_bar(gateway.health_score)
   230→
   231→            lines.append(
   232→                f"║ {status_icon} {name:12} │ {health_bar} │ {gateway.improvements_made:3d} improvements ║"
   233→            )
   234→
   235→        lines.extend(
   236→            [
   237→                "╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣",
   238→                f"║ Total Health: {self.get_master_status()['total_health']:.1%}                              ║",
   239→                "╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝",
   240→            ]
   241→        )
   242→
   243→        return "\n".join(lines)
   244→
   245→    def _render_health_bar(self, score: float, width: int = 20) -> str:
   246→        """Render ASCII health bar."""
   247→        filled = int(score * width)
   248→        empty = width - filled
   249→        return "█" * filled + "░" * empty + f" {score:.0%}"
   250→
   251→    def stop(self) -> None:
   252→        """Gracefully stop Omnibus."""
   253→        logger.info("🛑 Omnibus stopping...")
   254→        self.active = False
   255→        for squad in self.squads.values():
   256→            squad.stop()
   257→
   258→
   259→# Entry point
   260→async def awaken_omnibus():
   261→    """Awaken the Master Gateway Controller."""
   262→    omnibus = Omnibus()
   263→
   264→    # Print initial dashboard
   265→    print(omnibus.get_gateway_dashboard())
   266→
   267→    # Run forever
   268→    await omnibus.run_forever()
   269→
   270→
   271→if __name__ == "__main__":
   272→    asyncio.run(awaken_omnibus())
   273→

     1→"""Cache Gateway Squad - Optimizes L1/L2/L3 semantic caching.
     2→
     3→Unlocks the Cache Gateway through intelligent cache optimization.
     4→"""
     5→
     6→from __future__ import annotations
     7→
     8→import json
     9→import logging
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    11→from datetime import datetime
    12→from typing import Any
    13→
    14→from cohezion.research import ResearchAgent, ResearchConfig
    15→from cohezion.research.cost_optimization import CostBudget
    16→
    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→
    21→@dataclass
    22→class CacheMetrics:
    23→    """Metrics for cache performance."""
    24→
    25→    hit_rate: float
    26→    miss_rate: float
    27→    eviction_rate: float
    28→    avg_lookup_time_ms: float
    29→    memory_usage_mb: float
    30→    token_savings: int
    31→    timestamp: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().isoformat())
    32→
    33→
    34→class CacheGatewaySquad:
    35→    """Squad for optimizing semantic and token caching.
    36→
    37→    Targets:
    38→    - L1 cache (in-memory): Hit rate > 95%
    39→    - L2 cache (semantic): Hit rate > 85%
    40→    - L3 cache (persistent): Hit rate > 70%
    41→    """
    42→
    43→    def __init__(self):
    44→        """Initialize Cache Squad."""
    45→        self.agent = ResearchAgent(
    46→            config=ResearchConfig(
    47→                experiment_time_budget=300.0,
    48→                max_experiments=50,
    49→                target_metric="hit_rate",
    50→            )
    51→        )
    52→        self.improvements = []
    53→        logger.info("Cache Gateway Squad initialized")
    54→
    55→    def get_current_metrics(self) -> CacheMetrics:
    56→        """Get current cache metrics from the system."""
    57→        # In production: Query actual cache stats
    58→        # For now: Return realistic baseline
    59→        return CacheMetrics(
    60→            hit_rate=0.82,  # Below target
    61→            miss_rate=0.18,
    62→            eviction_rate=0.05,
    63→            avg_lookup_time_ms=12.0,
    64→            memory_usage_mb=512.0,
    65→            token_savings=1_000_000,
    66→        )
    67→
    68→    def detect_degradation(self, metrics: CacheMetrics) -> bool:
    69→        """Detect if cache needs optimization."""
    70→        issues = []
    71→
    72→        if metrics.hit_rate < 0.85:
    73→            issues.append(f"L2 hit rate {metrics.hit_rate:.1%} below target 85%")
    74→
    75→        if metrics.eviction_rate > 0.10:
    76→            issues.append(f"Eviction rate {metrics.eviction_rate:.1%} too high")
    77→
    78→        if metrics.avg_lookup_time_ms > 20.0:
    79→            issues.append(f"Lookup time {metrics.avg_lookup_time_ms:.1f}ms too slow")
    80→
    81→        if issues:
    82→            logger.warning(f"Cache degradation: {', '.join(issues)}")
    83→            return True
    84→
    85→        return False
    86→
    87→    def optimize_cache(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
    88→        """Run cache optimization experiments."""
    89→        logger.info("Running cache optimization...")
    90→
    91→        # Experiment with different cache sizes
    92→        experiments = [
    93→            {"cache_size_mb": 256, "ttl_seconds": 300},
    94→            {"cache_size_mb": 512, "ttl_seconds": 600},
    95→            {"cache_size_mb": 1024, "ttl_seconds": 900},
    96→        ]
    97→
    98→        best_config = None
    99→        best_score = 0.0
   100→
   101→        for config in experiments:
   102→            # Simulate cache performance with this config
   103→            score = self._evaluate_cache_config(config)
   104→
   105→            if score > best_score:
   106→                best_score = score
   107→                best_config = config
   108→
   109→        result = {
   110→            "optimized": best_score > 0.85,
   111→            "best_config": best_config,
   112→            "score": best_score,
   113→            "experiments": len(experiments),
   114→            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
   115→        }
   116→
   117→        self.improvements.append(result)
   118→        return result
   119→
   120→    def _evaluate_cache_config(self, config: dict[str, Any]) -> float:
   121→        """Evaluate a cache configuration."""
   122→        # Simulate hit rate based on cache size
   123→        # Larger cache = higher hit rate but diminishing returns
   124→        size = config["cache_size_mb"]
   125→
   126→        import math
   127→
   128→        hit_rate = 0.70 + 0.25 * (1 - math.exp(-size / 400))
   129→
   130→        # Penalize if too slow
   131→        ttl_penalty = config["ttl_seconds"] / 1000  # Longer TTL = more staleness
   132→
   133→        return hit_rate - (ttl_penalty * 0.01)
   134→
   135→    def get_report(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   136→        """Get cache squad report."""
   137→        return {
   138→            "squad": "cache",
   139→            "improvements_made": len(self.improvements),
   140→            "latest_improvement": self.improvements[-1] if self.improvements else None,
   141→            "health_score": 0.88 if self.improvements else 0.70,
   142→        }
   143→
   144→
   145→# Integration
   146→async def unlock_cache_gateway():
   147→    """Unlock the Cache Gateway."""
   148→    squad = CacheGatewaySquad()
   149→
   150→    # Check if optimization needed
   151→    metrics = squad.get_current_metrics()
   152→    if squad.detect_degradation(metrics):
   153→        result = squad.optimize_cache()
   154→        print(f"Cache Gateway: {result}")
   155→
   156→    return squad.get_report()
   157→
   158→
   159→if __name__ == "__main__":
   160→    import asyncio
   161→
   162→    asyncio.run(unlock_cache_gateway())
   163→

     1→"""Cost optimization for ResearchAgent.
     2→
     3→Token-aware budgeting, cost tracking, and budget enforcement.
     4→Elegant simplification with ~200 lines.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import json
    10→import logging
    11→from dataclasses import dataclass
    12→from datetime import datetime
    13→from pathlib import Path
    14→from typing import Any
    15→
    16→from cohezion.compound.models import ExecutionMetrics
    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→
    21→# Token cost assumptions (per 1K tokens)
    22→DEFAULT_COSTS = {
    23→    "ollama/phi3:mini": 0.0,  # Local = free
    24→    "ollama/llama3.1:8b": 0.0,  # Local = free
    25→    "ollama/qwen2.5:7b": 0.0,  # Local = free
    26→    "anthropic/claude-3-haiku": 0.25,  # $0.25 per 1K input tokens
    27→    "anthropic/claude-3-sonnet": 3.0,  # $3.00 per 1K input tokens
    28→    "openai/gpt-4o-mini": 0.15,  # $0.15 per 1K input tokens
    29→    "openai/gpt-4o": 2.5,  # $2.50 per 1K input tokens
    30→}
    31→
    32→
    33→@dataclass
    34→class CostBudget:
    35→    """Budget configuration for research sessions."""
    36→
    37→    max_cost_usd: float = 100.0  # Maximum cost per session
    38→    max_tokens: int = 1_000_000  # Maximum tokens per session
    39→    max_experiments: int = 100  # Maximum experiments
    40→    warning_threshold: float = 0.8  # Warn at 80% of budget
    41→    hard_limit: bool = True  # Hard stop at limit
    42→
    43→    def __post_init__(self):
    44→        """Validate budget parameters (Issue #6)."""
    45→        if self.max_cost_usd <= 0:
    46→            raise ValueError("max_cost_usd must be > 0")
    47→        if self.max_tokens <= 0:
    48→            raise ValueError("max_tokens must be > 0")
    49→        if self.max_experiments <= 0:
    50→            raise ValueError("max_experiments must be > 0")
    51→        if not (0.0 < self.warning_threshold <= 1.0):
    52→            raise ValueError("warning_threshold must be between 0.0 and 1.0")
    53→
    54→    def is_within_budget(
    55→        self,
    56→        current_cost: float,
    57→        current_tokens: int,
    58→        current_experiments: int,
    59→    ) -> tuple[bool, dict[str, Any]]:
    60→        """Check if within budget.
    61→
    62→        Args:
    63→            current_cost: Current cost in USD
    64→            current_tokens: Current token count
    65→            current_experiments: Current experiment count
    66→
    67→        Returns:
    68→            (within_budget, status_info)
    69→        """
    70→        status = {
    71→            "cost_ok": True,
    72→            "tokens_ok": True,
    73→            "experiments_ok": True,
    74→            "cost_percent": current_cost / self.max_cost_usd,
    75→            "token_percent": current_tokens / self.max_tokens,
    76→            "warning_triggered": False,
    77→        }
    78→
    79→        # Check cost
    80→        if current_cost >= self.max_cost_usd:
    81→            status["cost_ok"] = False
    82→            if self.hard_limit:
    83→                return False, status
    84→
    85→        # Check tokens
    86→        if current_tokens >= self.max_tokens:
    87→            status["tokens_ok"] = False
    88→            if self.hard_limit:
    89→                return False, status
    90→
    91→        # Check experiments
    92→        if current_experiments >= self.max_experiments:
    93→            status["experiments_ok"] = False
    94→            if self.hard_limit:
    95→                return False, status
    96→
    97→        # Check warning threshold
    98→        if (
    99→            status["cost_percent"] >= self.warning_threshold
   100→            or status["token_percent"] >= self.warning_threshold
   101→        ):
   102→            status["warning_triggered"] = True
   103→
   104→        return True, status
   105→
   106→
   107→@dataclass
   108→class ExperimentCost:
   109→    """Cost tracking for a single experiment."""
   110→
   111→    experiment_id: str
   112→    timestamp: str
   113→    tokens_used: int
   114→    cost_usd: float
   115→    model_used: str
   116→    duration_seconds: float
   117→
   118→    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   119→        """Convert to dictionary."""
   120→        return {
   121→            "experiment_id": self.experiment_id,
   122→            "timestamp": self.timestamp,
   123→            "tokens_used": self.tokens_used,
   124→            "cost_usd": round(self.cost_usd, 6),
   125→            "model_used": self.model_used,
   126→            "duration_seconds": self.duration_seconds,
   127→        }
   128→
   129→
   130→class CostTracker:
   131→    """Track costs across research sessions.
   132→
   133→    Token-aware budgeting with per-experiment tracking.
   134→    """
   135→
   136→    def __init__(
   137→        self,
   138→        budget: CostBudget | None = None,
   139→        costs_per_1k: dict[str, float] | None = None,
   140→        log_file: Path | None = None,
   141→    ):
   142→        """Initialize cost tracker.
   143→
   144→        Args:
   145→            budget: Budget configuration
   146→            costs_per_1k: Cost per 1K tokens per model
   147→            log_file: Path to cost log
   148→        """
   149→        self.budget = budget or CostBudget()
   150→        self.costs_per_1k = costs_per_1k or DEFAULT_COSTS.copy()
   151→        self.log_file = log_file or Path("data/research/costs.jsonl")
   152→        self.log_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   153→
   154→        # Running totals
   155→        self.total_cost = 0.0
   156→        self.total_tokens = 0
   157→        self.total_experiments = 0
   158→        self.experiment_costs: list[ExperimentCost] = []
   159→
   160→    def calculate_cost(
   161→        self,
   162→        tokens: int,
   163→        model: str = "ollama/phi3:mini",
   164→    ) -> float:
   165→        """Calculate cost for token usage.
   166→
   167→        Args:
   168→            tokens: Number of tokens
   169→            model: Model used
   170→
   171→        Returns:
   172→            Cost in USD
   173→        """
   174→        cost_per_1k = self.costs_per_1k.get(model, 0.0)
   175→        return (tokens / 1000) * cost_per_1k
   176→
   177→    def record_experiment(
   178→        self,
   179→        experiment_id: str,
   180→        metrics: ExecutionMetrics,
   181→        model: str = "ollama/phi3:mini",
   182→    ) -> ExperimentCost:
   183→        """Record experiment cost.
   184→
   185→        Args:
   186→            experiment_id: Experiment identifier
   187→            metrics: Execution metrics
   188→            model: Model used
   189→
   190→        Returns:
   191→            Experiment cost record
   192→        """
   193→        tokens = metrics.total_tokens or metrics.prompt_tokens + metrics.completion_tokens
   194→        cost = self.calculate_cost(tokens, model)
   195→
   196→        experiment_cost = ExperimentCost(
   197→            experiment_id=experiment_id,
   198→            timestamp=datetime.now().isoformat(),
   199→            tokens_used=tokens,
   200→            cost_usd=cost,
   201→            model_used=model,
   202→            duration_seconds=metrics.duration_seconds,
   203→        )
   204→
   205→        # Update totals
   206→        self.total_cost += cost
   207→        self.total_tokens += tokens
   208→        self.total_experiments += 1
   209→        self.experiment_costs.append(experiment_cost)
   210→
   211→        # Log to file
   212→        with open(self.log_file, "a") as f:
   213→            f.write(json.dumps(experiment_cost.to_dict()) + "\n")
   214→
   215→        logger.info(
   216→            f"Experiment {experiment_id}: {tokens} tokens, ${cost:.6f} "
   217→            f"(total: ${self.total_cost:.6f})"
   218→        )
   219→
   220→        return experiment_cost
   221→
   222→    def check_budget(self) -> tuple[bool, dict[str, Any]]:
   223→        """Check if within budget.
   224→
   225→        Returns:
   226→            (within_budget, status_info)
   227→        """
   228→        return self.budget.is_within_budget(
   229→            self.total_cost,
   230→            self.total_tokens,
   231→            self.total_experiments,
   232→        )
   233→
   234→    def get_cost_report(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   235→        """Get comprehensive cost report.
   236→
   237→        Returns:
   238→            Cost report dictionary
   239→        """
   240→        within_budget, status = self.check_budget()
   241→
   242→        # Calculate per-model costs
   243→        model_costs: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
   244→        for exp in self.experiment_costs:
   245→            if exp.model_used not in model_costs:
   246→                model_costs[exp.model_used] = {
   247→                    "tokens": 0,
   248→                    "cost": 0.0,
   249→                    "experiments": 0,
   250→                }
   251→            model_costs[exp.model_used]["tokens"] += exp.tokens_used
   252→            model_costs[exp.model_used]["cost"] += exp.cost_usd
   253→            model_costs[exp.model_used]["experiments"] += 1
   254→
   255→        return {
   256→            "total_cost_usd": round(self.total_cost, 6),
   257→            "total_tokens": self.total_tokens,
   258→            "total_experiments": self.total_experiments,
   259→            "budget": {
   260→                "max_cost_usd": self.budget.max_cost_usd,
   261→                "max_tokens": self.budget.max_tokens,
   262→                "max_experiments": self.budget.max_experiments,
   263→            },
   264→            "usage_percent": {
   265→                "cost": round(self.total_cost / self.budget.max_cost_usd * 100, 2),
   266→                "tokens": round(self.total_tokens / self.budget.max_tokens * 100, 2),
   267→                "experiments": round(self.total_experiments / self.budget.max_experiments * 100, 2),
   268→            },
   269→            "within_budget": within_budget,
   270→            "status": status,
   271→            "per_model": model_costs,
   272→            "avg_cost_per_experiment": round(self.total_cost / max(1, self.total_experiments), 6),
   273→            "avg_tokens_per_experiment": round(
   274→                self.total_tokens / max(1, self.total_experiments), 2
   275→            ),
   276→        }
   277→
   278→    def get_experiment_costs(self) -> list[ExperimentCost]:
   279→        """Get all experiment costs.
   280→
   281→        Returns:
   282→            List of experiment costs
   283→        """
   284→        return self.experiment_costs.copy()
   285→
   286→    def export_costs_csv(self, path: Path) -> None:
   287→        """Export costs to CSV.
   288→
   289→        Args:
   290→            path: Output CSV path
   291→        """
   292→        import csv
   293→
   294→        with open(path, "w", newline="") as f:
   295→            writer = csv.writer(f)
   296→            writer.writerow(
   297→                [
   298→                    "experiment_id",
   299→                    "timestamp",
   300→                    "tokens_used",
   301→                    "cost_usd",
   302→                    "model_used",
   303→                    "duration_seconds",
   304→                ]
   305→            )
   306→            for exp in self.experiment_costs:
   307→                writer.writerow(
   308→                    [
   309→                        exp.experiment_id,
   310→                        exp.timestamp,
   311→                        exp.tokens_used,
   312→                        exp.cost_usd,
   313→                        exp.model_used,
   314→                        exp.duration_seconds,
   315→                    ]
   316→                )
   317→
   318→        logger.info(f"Cost data exported to {path}")
   319→
   320→
   321→class CostAwareRouter:
   322→    """Route experiments to models based on cost constraints.
   323→
   324→    Automatically downgrades models when budget is tight.
   325→    """
   326→
   327→    def __init__(
   328→        self,
   329→        cost_tracker: CostTracker,
   330→        cost_threshold: float = 0.9,
   331→    ):
   332→        """Initialize cost-aware router.
   333→
   334→        Args:
   335→            cost_tracker: Cost tracker instance
   336→            cost_threshold: Threshold to switch to cheaper models
   337→        """
   338→        self.cost_tracker = cost_tracker
   339→        self.cost_threshold = cost_threshold
   340→
   341→        # Model tiers
   342→        self.tiers = [
   343→            ["ollama/phi3:mini"],  # Free tier
   344→            ["ollama/llama3.1:8b", "ollama/qwen2.5:7b"],  # Local tier
   345→            ["anthropic/claude-3-haiku", "openai/gpt-4o-mini"],  # Cheap API tier
   346→            ["anthropic/claude-3-sonnet", "openai/gpt-4o"],  # Expensive tier
   347→        ]
   348→
   349→    def select_model(
   350→        self,
   351→        preferred_model: str,
   352→        complexity: float = 0.5,
   353→    ) -> str:
   354→        """Select model based on cost constraints.
   355→
   356→        Args:
   357→            preferred_model: Preferred model
   358→            complexity: Task complexity 0-1
   359→
   360→        Returns:
   361→            Selected model
   362→        """
   363→        # Check budget status
   364→        within_budget, status = self.cost_tracker.check_budget()
   365→        cost_percent = status.get("cost_percent", 0.0)
   366→
   367→        # If under threshold, use preferred
   368→        if cost_percent < self.cost_threshold:
   369→            return preferred_model
   370→
   371→        # Find current tier
   372→        current_tier = 0
   373→        for i, tier in enumerate(self.tiers):
   374→            if preferred_model in tier:
   375→                current_tier = i
   376→                break
   377→
   378→        # Downgrade to cheaper tier
   379→        if current_tier > 0:
   380→            cheaper_tier = self.tiers[current_tier - 1]
   381→            # Pick based on complexity
   382→            if complexity > 0.7 and len(cheaper_tier) > 1:
   383→                selected = cheaper_tier[1]  # More capable in tier
   384→            else:
   385→                selected = cheaper_tier[0]  # Cheapest in tier
   386→
   387→            logger.warning(
   388→                f"Budget at {cost_percent:.1%}, downgraded {preferred_model} to {selected}"
   389→            )
   390→            return selected
   391→
   392→        return preferred_model  # Already at cheapest
   393→
   394→    def should_continue(self) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   395→        """Check if should continue experiments.
   396→
   397→        Returns:
   398→            (should_continue, reason)
   399→        """
   400→        within_budget, status = self.cost_tracker.check_budget()
   401→
   402→        if not within_budget:
   403→            if not status.get("cost_ok", True):
   404→                return False, "Cost budget exceeded"
   405→            if not status.get("tokens_ok", True):
   406→                return False, "Token budget exceeded"
   407→            if not status.get("experiments_ok", True):
   408→                return False, "Experiment limit reached"
   409→
   410→        if status.get("warning_triggered", False):
   411→            return True, "Budget warning: consider reducing model cost"
   412→
   413→        return True, "Within budget"
   414→
   415→
   416→# Integration with ResearchAgent
   417→def create_cost_tracker(
   418→    max_cost: float = 100.0,
   419→    max_tokens: int = 1_000_000,
   420→    max_experiments: int = 100,
   421→) -> CostTracker:
   422→    """Factory for creating cost tracker.
   423→
   424→    Args:
   425→        max_cost: Maximum cost in USD
   426→        max_tokens: Maximum tokens
   427→        max_experiments: Maximum experiments
   428→
   429→    Returns:
   430→        Configured CostTracker
   431→    """
   432→    budget = CostBudget(
   433→        max_cost_usd=max_cost,
   434→        max_tokens=max_tokens,
   435→        max_experiments=max_experiments,
   436→    )
   437→    return CostTracker(budget=budget)
   438→
   439→
   440→def integrate_with_research_agent(agent: Any) -> CostTracker:
   441→    """Integrate cost tracking with ResearchAgent.
   442→
   443→    Args:
   444→        agent: ResearchAgent instance
   445→
   446→    Returns:
   447→        Configured CostTracker
   448→    """
   449→    tracker = create_cost_tracker(
   450→        max_cost=agent.config.max_experiments * 0.5,  # $0.50 per experiment
   451→        max_experiments=agent.config.max_experiments,
   452→    )
   453→
   454→    # Store on agent
   455→    agent.cost_tracker = tracker
   456→    agent.cost_router = CostAwareRouter(tracker)
   457→
   458→    logger.info(f"Cost tracking integrated: budget ${tracker.budget.max_cost_usd:.2f}")
   459→    return tracker
   460→
   461→
   462→# Cost optimization utilities
   463→def estimate_experiment_cost(
   464→    model: str,
   465→    tokens: int = 1000,
   466→    costs_per_1k: dict[str, float] | None = None,
   467→) -> float:
   468→    """Estimate cost for an experiment.
   469→
   470→    Args:
   471→        model: Model to use
   472→        tokens: Expected tokens
   473→        costs_per_1k: Cost per 1K tokens
   474→
   475→    Returns:
   476→        Estimated cost in USD
   477→    """
   478→    costs = costs_per_1k or DEFAULT_COSTS
   479→    cost_per_1k = costs.get(model, 0.0)
   480→    return (tokens / 1000) * cost_per_1k
   481→
   482→
   483→def get_cheapest_model(models: list[str]) -> str:
   484→    """Get cheapest model from list.
   485→
   486→    Args:
   487→        models: List of model names
   488→
   489→    Returns:
   490→        Cheapest model
   491→    """
   492→    return min(models, key=lambda m: DEFAULT_COSTS.get(m, float("inf")))
   493→
   494→
   495→def calculate_session_budget(
   496→    num_experiments: int,
   497→    avg_tokens_per_experiment: int = 1000,
   498→    model: str = "ollama/phi3:mini",
   499→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   500→    """Calculate expected budget for a session.
   501→
   502→    Args:
   503→        num_experiments: Number of experiments
   504→        avg_tokens_per_experiment: Average tokens per experiment
   505→        model: Default model
   506→
   507→    Returns:
   508→        Budget estimate
   509→    """
   510→    cost_per_exp = estimate_experiment_cost(model, avg_tokens_per_experiment)
   511→    total_cost = cost_per_exp * num_experiments
   512→    total_tokens = avg_tokens_per_experiment * num_experiments
   513→
   514→    return {
   515→        "num_experiments": num_experiments,
   516→        "estimated_cost_usd": round(total_cost, 2),
   517→        "estimated_tokens": total_tokens,
   518→        "model": model,
   519→        "cost_per_experiment": round(cost_per_exp, 4),
   520→        "recommendation": "OK" if total_cost < 50 else "Consider cheaper model",
   521→    }
   522→

     1→"""Autonomous research agent for training optimization.
     2→
     3→Elegant integration with Cohezion compound executor.
     4→Based on karpathy/autoresearch patterns.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import json
    10→import logging
    11→import subprocess
    12→import time
    13→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    14→from datetime import datetime
    15→from pathlib import Path
    16→from typing import Any
    17→
    18→from cohezion.compound.core.executor import CompoundExecutor, ExecutionConfig
    19→from cohezion.compound.models import ExecutionResult, Task
    20→from cohezion.reliability import get_circuit  # Issue #8
    21→from cohezion.research.config import ExperimentResult, ResearchConfig
    22→
    23→
    24→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass
    28→class ResearchSession:
    29→    """Session state for research.
    30→
    31→    Minimal state tracking, delegates to Cohezion persistence.
    32→    """
    33→
    34→    session_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().isoformat())
    35→    experiments_completed: int = 0
    36→    best_metric: float = float("inf")
    37→    best_checkpoint: Path | None = None
    38→    active: bool = True
    39→
    40→
    41→class ResearchAgent:
    42→    """Autonomous research agent.
    43→
    44→    Clean implementation (~200 lines) following elegant simplification.
    45→    Integrates with Cohezion's compound executor and metrics systems.
    46→
    47→    Inspired by karpathy/autoresearch but integrated into Cohezion ecosystem.
    48→    """
    49→
    50→    def __init__(
    51→        self,
    52→        config: ResearchConfig | None = None,
    53→        executor: CompoundExecutor | None = None,
    54→    ):
    55→        """Initialize research agent.
    56→
    57→        Args:
    58→            config: Research configuration
    59→            executor: CompoundExecutor for running experiments
    60→                     (creates new one if None)
    61→        """
    62→        self.config = config or ResearchConfig()
    63→        self.session = ResearchSession()
    64→
    65→        # Create executor if not provided
    66→        if executor is None:
    67→            executor = CompoundExecutor(
    68→                execute_fn=self._run_experiment_with_circuit_breaker,  # Issue #8
    69→                config=ExecutionConfig(max_retries=1),
    70→            )
    71→        self.executor = executor
    72→
    73→        # Circuit breaker for reliability (Issue #8)
    74→        self.circuit = get_circuit("research_agent", failure_threshold=5, recovery_timeout=60)
    75→
    76→        logger.info(f"ResearchAgent initialized: {self.session.session_id}")
    77→
    78→    def _run_experiment_with_circuit_breaker(
    79→        self,
    80→        task: Task,
    81→        context: dict[str, Any],
    82→    ) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    83→        """Run experiment with circuit breaker protection (Issue #8)."""
    84→        # Check if circuit is open
    85→        if self.circuit.is_open():
    86→            logger.warning("Circuit breaker open, skipping experiment")
    87→            return "Circuit open", {"error": "Circuit breaker open", "skipped": True}
    88→
    89→        try:
    90→            result = self._run_experiment(task, context)
    91→            self.circuit.record_success()
    92→            return result
    93→        except Exception as e:
    94→            self.circuit.record_failure()
    95→            logger.error(f"Experiment failed, circuit breaker recorded: {e}")
    96→            raise
    97→        self,
    98→        task: Task,
    99→        context: dict[str, Any],
   100→    ) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   101→        """Run single experiment.
   102→
   103→        This is the core execution function passed to CompoundExecutor.
   104→        """
   105→        experiment_id = task.id
   106→        start_time = time.time()
   107→
   108→        logger.info(f"Running experiment: {experiment_id}")
   109→
   110→        try:
   111→            # Run training with time budget
   112→            result = self._execute_training(
   113→                time_budget=self.config.experiment_time_budget,
   114→            )
   115→
   116→            # Parse result
   117→            metric_value = result.get("val_bpb", float("inf"))
   118→            duration = time.time() - start_time
   119→
   120→            # Check if improved
   121→            improved = metric_value < self.session.best_metric
   122→            if improved:
   123→                self.session.best_metric = metric_value
   124→                logger.info(f"New best metric: {metric_value:.4f}")
   125→
   126→            return (
   127→                f"Experiment {experiment_id}: {metric_value:.4f}",
   128→                {
   129→                    "metric_value": metric_value,
   130→                    "duration": duration,
   131→                    "improved": improved,
   132→                },
   133→            )
   134→
   135→        except Exception as e:
   136→            logger.error(f"Experiment {experiment_id} failed: {e}")
   137→            return (
   138→                f"Failed: {e}",
   139→                {
   140→                    "metric_value": float("inf"),
   141→                    "duration": time.time() - start_time,
   142→                    "improved": False,
   143→                },
   144→            )
   145→
   146→    def _execute_training(self, time_budget: float) -> dict[str, Any]:
   147→        """Execute training with time limit.
   148→
   149→        Runs autoresearch-style training for fixed duration.
   150→        """
   151→        # Resolve train script relative to project root
   152→        project_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent
   153→        train_script = (project_root / self.config.train_file).resolve()
   154→        if not str(train_script).startswith(str(project_root)):
   155→            raise ValueError(f"Train script outside project root: {self.config.train_file}")
   156→
   157→        # Run security guardrails if enabled
   158→        if self.config.enable_guardrails and train_script.exists():
   159→            from cohezion.research.security import CodeChange, ResearchSecurityGuardrails
   160→
   161→            guardrails = ResearchSecurityGuardrails()
   162→            change = CodeChange(
   163→                file_path=train_script,
   164→                old_code="",
   165→                new_code=train_script.read_text(),
   166→                change_type="modify",
   167→            )
   168→            validation = guardrails.validate_change(change)
   169→            if not validation.is_valid:
   170→                raise RuntimeError(f"Guardrail blocked execution: {validation.issues}")
   171→
   172→        result = subprocess.run(
   173→            [
   174→                "python",
   175→                str(train_script),
   176→                "--time_budget",
   177→                str(time_budget),
   178→            ],
   179→            capture_output=True,
   180→            text=True,
   181→            timeout=time_budget + 60,  # Safety margin
   182→        )
   183→
   184→        if result.returncode != 0:
   185→            raise RuntimeError(f"Training failed: {result.stderr}")
   186→
   187→        # Parse metrics from output
   188→        # (simplified - in real implementation would parse JSON)
   189→        return {
   190→            "val_bpb": 2.5,  # placeholder
   191→            "train_loss": 2.3,
   192→            "val_loss": 2.4,
   193→        }
   194→
   195→    def run_session(
   196→        self,
   197→        max_experiments: int | None = None,
   198→    ) -> ResearchSession:
   199→        """Run complete research session.
   200→
   201→        Args:
   202→            max_experiments: Override default max
   203→
   204→        Returns:
   205→            Final session state
   206→        """
   207→        max_exp = max_experiments or self.config.max_experiments
   208→
   209→        logger.info(f"Starting research session: {max_exp} experiments")
   210→
   211→        while self.session.experiments_completed < max_exp and self.session.active:
   212→            # Create experiment task
   213→            exp_id = f"exp-{self.session.experiments_completed + 1}"
   214→            task = Task(
   215→                id=exp_id,
   216→                description=f"Research experiment {exp_id}",
   217→                skill_name="research",
   218→                operation_type="optimize",
   219→            )
   220→
   221→            # Run via executor
   222→            result = self.executor.execute(task)
   223→
   224→            # Log result
   225→            self._log_experiment(exp_id, result)
   226→
   227→            # Update session
   228→            self.session.experiments_completed += 1
   229→
   230→            logger.info(
   231→                f"Experiment {exp_id} complete: {self.session.experiments_completed}/{max_exp}"
   232→            )
   233→
   234→        logger.info(f"Research session complete: {self.session}")
   235→        return self.session
   236→
   237→    def _log_experiment(self, exp_id: str, result: ExecutionResult) -> None:
   238→        """Log experiment result."""
   239→        # Normalize metrics — handle both ExecutionMetrics objects and legacy dicts
   240→        m = result.metrics
   241→        if isinstance(m, dict):
   242→            metric_value = m.get("metric_value", float("inf"))
   243→            improved = m.get("improved", False)
   244→            duration_seconds = m.get("duration_seconds", 0.0)
   245→        else:
   246→            metric_value = getattr(m, "metric_value", float("inf"))
   247→            improved = getattr(m, "improved", False)
   248→            duration_seconds = m.duration_seconds
   249→
   250→        exp_result = ExperimentResult(
   251→            experiment_id=exp_id,
   252→            timestamp=datetime.now().isoformat(),
   253→            metric_value=metric_value,
   254→            metric_name=self.config.target_metric,
   255→            improved=improved,
   256→            code_changes=[],  # Would track actual changes
   257→            duration_seconds=duration_seconds,
   258→        )
   259→
   260→        # Append to experiment log
   261→        with open(self.config.experiment_log, "a") as f:
   262→            f.write(json.dumps(exp_result.to_dict()) + "\n")
   263→
   264→    def get_best_result(self) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   265→        """Get best experiment result from session."""
   266→        if self.session.best_metric == float("inf"):
   267→            return None
   268→
   269→        return {
   270→            "metric": self.session.best_metric,
   271→            "checkpoint": str(self.session.best_checkpoint),
   272→            "experiments": self.session.experiments_completed,
   273→        }
   274→
   275→    def stop(self) -> None:
   276→        """Stop research session gracefully."""
   277→        self.session.active = False
   278→        logger.info("Research session stopped")
   279→
